[31m+ async function askAssistant(input: {[39m
[31m+   messages: Array<{ role: "user" | "assistant"; content: string }>;[39m
[31m+   contactId?: number;[39m
[31m+ }) {[39m
[31m+   const response = await fetch("/api/assistant", {[39m
[31m+     method: "POST",[39m
[31m+     credentials: "include",[39m
[31m+     headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },[39m
[31m+     body: JSON.stringify(input),[39m
[31m+   });[39m
[31m+   const body = (await response.json().catch(() => ({}))) as AssistantResponse;[39m
[31m+   if (!response.ok)[39m
[31m+     throw new Error(body.error || "AmarktAI could not respond.");[39m
[31m+   return body;[39m
[31m+ }[39m

[31m+[39m
[31m+ function SecondFactorGate({[39m
[31m+   hasEmail,[39m
[31m+   smtpReady,[39m
[31m+ }: {[39m
[31m+   hasEmail: boolean;[39m
[31m+   smtpReady: boolean;[39m
[31m+ }) {[39m
[31m+   const [requested, setRequested] = useState(false);[39m
[31m+   const [code, setCode] = useState("");[39m
[31m+   const [feedback, setFeedback] = useState("");[39m
[31m+   const status = trpc.security.status.useQuery();[39m
[31m+   const requestCode = trpc.security.requestEmailCode.useMutation({[39m
[31m+     onSuccess: () => {[39m
[31m+       setRequested(true);[39m
[31m+       setFeedback("");[39m
[31m+       toast.success("Verification code sent.");[39m
[31m+     },[39m
[31m+     onError: () => setFeedback("The code could not be sent. Please try again."),[39m
[31m+   });[39m
[31m+   const verifyCode = trpc.security.verifyEmailCode.useMutation({[39m
[31m+     onSuccess: () => {[39m
[31m+       setFeedback("");[39m
[31m+       status.refetch();[39m
[31m+       window.location.assign("/dashboard");[39m
[31m+     },[39m
[31m+     onError: () =>[39m
[31m+       setFeedback([39m
[31m+         "That code wasn’t accepted. Check the six digits or request a new one."[39m
[31m+       ),[39m
[31m+   });[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   return ([39m
[31m+     <div className="grid min-h-screen place-items-center bg-[#F5F7FA] p-5 text-[#26354A]">[39m
[31m+       <div className="w-full max-w-lg rounded-2xl border border-[#DCE2E9] bg-white p-7 shadow-sm sm:p-8">[39m
[31m+         <BrandMark />[39m
[31m+         <div className="mt-8 flex items-center gap-3">[39m
[31m+           <span className="grid size-10 place-items-center rounded-xl bg-[#EDF3FF] text-[#315BB6]">[39m
[31m+             <LockKeyhole size={19} />[39m
[31m+           </span>[39m
[31m+           <div>[39m
[31m+             <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-[.12em] text-[#6C798B]">[39m
[31m+               Security check[39m
[31m+             </p>[39m
[31m+             <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold">Confirm access</h1>[39m
[31m+           </div>[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+         {feedback ? ([39m
[31m+           <p[39m
[31m+             role="alert"[39m
[31m+             className="mt-5 rounded-xl border border-red-300 bg-red-50 p-4 text-sm font-semibold text-red-900"[39m
[31m+           >[39m
[31m+             {feedback}[39m
[31m+           </p>[39m
[31m+         ) : null}[39m
[31m+         {!hasEmail ? ([39m
[31m+           <Notice text="This account needs an email address before access can be verified." />[39m
[31m+         ) : !smtpReady ? ([39m
[31m+           <Notice text="Email verification is not available yet. Ask the administrator to finish email setup." />[39m
[31m+         ) : !requested ? ([39m
[31m+           <>[39m
[31m+             <p className="mt-4 text-sm leading-6 text-[#6C798B]">[39m
[31m+               We’ll send a six-digit code to your account email.[39m
[31m+             </p>[39m
[31m+             <Button[39m
[31m+               onClick={() => requestCode.mutate()}[39m
[31m+               disabled={requestCode.isPending}[39m
[31m+               className="mt-5 h-12 w-full"[39m
[31m+             >[39m
[31m+               <MailCheck className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" />[39m
[31m+               {requestCode.isPending ? "Sending…" : "Send code"}[39m
[31m+             </Button>[39m
[31m+           </>[39m
[31m+         ) : ([39m
[31m+           <>[39m
[31m+             <p className="mt-4 text-sm leading-6 text-[#6C798B]">[39m
[31m+               Enter the six-digit code.[39m
[31m+             </p>[39m
[31m+             <input[39m
[31m+               inputMode="numeric"[39m
[31m+               maxLength={6}[39m
[31m+               value={code}[39m
[31m+               onChange={event => setCode(event.target.value.replace(/\D/g, ""))}[39m
[31m+               placeholder="000000"[39m
[31m+               className="mt-5 h-14 w-full rounded-xl border-2 border-[#D5DDE7] text-center text-2xl font-bold tracking-[.3em] outline-none focus:border-[#6F91E2]"[39m
[31m+             />[39m
[31m+             <Button[39m
[31m+               onClick={() => verifyCode.mutate({ code })}[39m
[31m+               disabled={code.length !== 6 || verifyCode.isPending}[39m
[31m+               className="mt-4 h-12 w-full"[39m
[31m+             >[39m
[31m+               {verifyCode.isPending ? "Checking…" : "Continue"}[39m
[31m+             </Button>[39m
[31m+             <button[39m
[31m+               onClick={() => requestCode.mutate()}[39m
[31m+               className="mt-4 w-full text-sm font-bold text-[#3F70D8]"[39m
[31m+             >[39m
[31m+               Send a new code[39m
[31m+             </button>[39m
[31m+           </>[39m
[31m+         )}[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+     </div>[39m
[31m+   );[39m
[31m+ }[39m

[31m+[39m
[31m+ type StreamMessage =[39m
[31m+   | { type: "ready"; url: string; control: string; expiresAt: string }[39m
[31m+   | { type: "frame"; data: string; url: string; metadata?: FrameMetadata }[39m
[31m+   | { type: "navigation"; url: string }[39m
[31m+   | { type: "control"; control: string; message?: string }[39m
[31m+   | { type: "pong"; expiresAt?: string }[39m
[31m+   | { type: "error"; code: string; message?: string }[39m
[31m+   | { type: "disconnected"; message: string }[39m
[31m+   | {[39m
[31m+       type: "session";[39m
[31m+       currentUrl: string;[39m
[31m+       authenticationState: string;[39m
[31m+       connectionHealth: string;[39m
[31m+       errorMessage?: string;[39m
[31m+       blockedDestination?: string;[39m
[31m+     };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ function streamUrl(session: ViewerReady) {[39m
[31m+   const protocol = window.location.protocol === "https:" ? "wss:" : "ws:";[39m
[31m+   const url = new URL([39m
[31m+     `${protocol}//${window.location.host}/api/crm-viewer/stream`[39m
[31m+   );[39m
[31m+   url.searchParams.set("session", session.viewerSessionId);[39m
[31m+   url.searchParams.set("token", session.viewerToken);[39m
[31m+   return url.toString();[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ export default function CrmWorkspace() {[39m
[31m+   const [, params] = useRoute("/crm/:connectedSystemId");[39m
[31m+   const [, navigate] = useLocation();[39m
[31m+   const organisation = trpc.organisation.current.useQuery();[39m
[31m+   const utils = trpc.useUtils();[39m
[31m+   const canManage =[39m
[31m+     organisation.data?.role === "owner" ||[39m
[31m+     organisation.data?.role === "manager";[39m
[31m+   const companySetup = trpc.companySetup.get.useQuery(undefined, {[39m
[31m+     enabled: Boolean(canManage),[39m
[31m+     retry: false,[39m
[31m+   });[39m
[31m+   const completeOnboarding = trpc.organisation.updateOnboarding.useMutation();[39m
[31m+   const systems = trpc.connectedSystems.list.useQuery([39m
[31m+     { organisationId: organisation.data?.organisationId || 0 },[39m
[31m+     {[39m
[31m+       enabled: Boolean(organisation.data?.organisationId),[39m
[31m+       refetchInterval: 3_000,[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+   );[39m
[31m+   const [selectedId, setSelectedId] = useState<number | null>([39m
[31m+     params?.connectedSystemId ? Number(params.connectedSystemId) : null[39m
[31m+   );[39m
[31m+   const [browserAuthenticationState, setBrowserAuthenticationState] =[39m
[31m+     useState("STARTING");[39m
[31m+   const [commissioningReady, setCommissioningReady] = useState(false);[39m
[31m+   const completionAttemptedRef = useRef(false);[39m
[31m+   const selected = useMemo([39m
[31m+     () =>[39m
[31m+       systems.data?.find(system => system.id === selectedId) ||[39m
[31m+       systems.data?.find(system => system.baseUrl) ||[39m
[31m+       null,[39m
[31m+     [selectedId, systems.data][39m
[31m+   );[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => {[39m
[31m+     if (selected && selected.id !== selectedId) setSelectedId(selected.id);[39m
[31m+   }, [selected, selectedId]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const onboarding = organisation.data?.settings?.onboarding;[39m
[31m+   const onboardingComplete = Boolean([39m
[31m+     onboarding &&[39m
[31m+       typeof onboarding === "object" &&[39m
[31m+       !Array.isArray(onboarding) &&[39m
[31m+       (onboarding as { complete?: unknown }).complete === true[39m
[31m+   );[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => {[39m
[31m+     setCommissioningReady(false);[39m
[31m+     setBrowserAuthenticationState("STARTING");[39m
[31m+     completionAttemptedRef.current = false;[39m
[31m+     if (!selected || !canManage || onboardingComplete) return;[39m
[31m+     let cancelled = false;[39m
[31m+     const check = async () => {[39m
[31m+       const response = await fetch([39m
[31m+         `/api/connected-system-admin/${selected.id}/commissioning`,[39m
[31m+         { credentials: "include" }[39m
[31m+       );[39m
[31m+       if (!response.ok || cancelled) return;[39m
[31m+       const body = (await response.json().catch(() => ({}))) as {[39m
[31m+         job?: { state?: string; status?: string } | null;[39m
[31m+       };[39m
[31m+       setCommissioningReady([39m
[31m+         body.job?.state === "READY" && body.job?.status === "ready"[39m
[31m+       );[39m
[31m+     };[39m
[31m+     void check();[39m
[31m+     const timer = window.setInterval(() => void check(), 3_000);[39m
[31m+     return () => {[39m
[31m+       cancelled = true;[39m
[31m+       window.clearInterval(timer);[39m
[31m+     };[39m
[31m+   }, [canManage, onboardingComplete, selected]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => {[39m
[31m+     if ([39m
[31m+       !canManage ||[39m
[31m+       onboardingComplete ||[39m
[31m+       completionAttemptedRef.current ||[39m
[31m+       browserAuthenticationState !== "AUTHENTICATED" ||[39m
[31m+       !commissioningReady ||[39m
[31m+       companySetup.data?.profile?.discoveryStatus !== "confirmed"[39m
[31m+     )[39m
[31m+       return;[39m
[31m+     completionAttemptedRef.current = true;[39m
[31m+     void completeOnboarding[39m
[31m+       .mutateAsync({ step: 4, complete: true })[39m
[31m+       .then(async () => {[39m
[31m+         await utils.organisation.current.invalidate();[39m
[31m+         toast.success("Setup complete. Your Assistant is ready.");[39m
[31m+         navigate("/assistant");[39m
[31m+       })[39m
[31m+       .catch(error => {[39m
[31m+         completionAttemptedRef.current = false;[39m
[31m+         toast.error([39m
[31m+           friendlyError([39m
[31m+             error,[39m
[31m+             "CRM commissioning is not fully proven yet, or completion could not be saved. Finish the required CRM operations and try again."[39m
[31m+           )[39m
[31m+         );[39m
[31m+       });[39m
[31m+   }, [[39m
[31m+     browserAuthenticationState,[39m
[31m+     canManage,[39m
[31m+     companySetup.data?.profile?.discoveryStatus,[39m
[31m+     completeOnboarding,[39m
[31m+     onboardingComplete,[39m
[31m+     navigate,[39m
[31m+     commissioningReady,[39m
[31m+     utils.organisation.current,[39m
[31m+   ]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   return ([39m
[31m+     <DashboardLayout>[39m
[31m+       <div[39m
[31m+         data-crm-workspace-root[39m
[31m+         className="h-[calc(100vh-66px)] min-h-0 overflow-hidden bg-[#EDF2F7]"[39m
[31m+       >[39m
[31m+         <style>{`[39m
[31m+           main:has(> [data-crm-workspace-root]) {[39m
[31m+             height: calc(100vh - 66px) !important;[39m
[31m+             min-height: 0 !important;[39m
[31m+             overflow: hidden !important;[39m
[31m+             padding: 0 !important;[39m
[31m+           }[39m
[31m+           main:has(> [data-crm-workspace-root]) > div:not([data-crm-workspace-root]) {[39m
[31m+             display: none !important;[39m
[31m+           }[39m
[31m+         `}</style>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+         {selected ? ([39m
[31m+           <LiveWorkspace[39m
[31m+             key={selected.id}[39m
[31m+             connectedSystemId={selected.id}[39m
[31m+             crmName={selected.displayName}[39m
[31m+             systems={systems.data ?? []}[39m
[31m+             onChoose={id => navigate(`/crm/${id}`)}[39m
[31m+             onToday={() => navigate("/today")}[39m
[31m+             onAuthenticationState={setBrowserAuthenticationState}[39m
[31m+           />[39m
[31m+         ) : ([39m
[31m+           <NoBrowserCrm onConnections={() => navigate("/connections")} />[39m
[31m+         )}[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+     </DashboardLayout>[39m
[31m+   );[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ function LiveWorkspace({[39m
[31m+   connectedSystemId,[39m
[31m+   crmName,[39m
[31m+   systems,[39m
[31m+   onChoose,[39m
[31m+   onToday,[39m
[31m+   onAuthenticationState,[39m
[31m+ }: {[39m
[31m+   connectedSystemId: number;[39m
[31m+   crmName: string;[39m
[31m+   systems: Array<{ id: number; displayName: string; baseUrl?: string | null }>;[39m
[31m+   onChoose: (id: number) => void;[39m
[31m+   onToday: () => void;[39m
[31m+   onAuthenticationState: (state: string) => void;[39m
[31m+ }) {[39m
[31m+   const open = trpc.crmViewer.open.useMutation();[39m
[31m+   const acquireAi = trpc.crmViewer.acquireAssistantControl.useMutation();[39m
[31m+   const releaseAi = trpc.crmViewer.releaseAssistantControl.useMutation();[39m
[31m+   const askAssistant = trpc.crmViewer.askAssistant.useMutation();[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const [session, setSession] = useState<ViewerReady | null>(null);[39m
[31m+   const [control, setControl] = useState("IDLE");[39m
[31m+   const [assistantOpen, setAssistantOpen] = useState(false);[39m
[31m+   const [image, setImage] = useState("");[39m
[31m+   const [frameMetadata, setFrameMetadata] = useState<FrameMetadata | null>([39m
[31m+     null[39m
[31m+   );[39m
[31m+   const [status, setStatus] = useState("Opening your CRM…");[39m
[31m+   const [currentUrl, setCurrentUrl] = useState("");[39m
[31m+   const [authenticationState, setAuthenticationState] = useState("STARTING");[39m
[31m+   const [assistantPrompt, setAssistantPrompt] = useState("");[39m
[31m+   const [assistantResult, setAssistantResult] = useState<string | null>(null);[39m
[31m+   const [assistantWorkflowRunId, setAssistantWorkflowRunId] = useState<number | null>(null);[39m
[31m+   const [activity, setActivity] = useState<string[]>([[39m
[31m+     "Secure CRM workspace opened",[39m
[31m+   ]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const viewerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);[39m
[31m+   const socketRef = useRef<WebSocket | null>(null);[39m
[31m+   const controlRef = useRef(control);[39m
[31m+   const pendingInputsRef = useRef<BrowserInputEvent[]>([]);[39m
[31m+   const pendingNavigationRef = useRef<BrowserNavigationAction | null>(null);[39m
[31m+   const humanControlRequestedRef = useRef(false);[39m
[31m+   const pendingAiControlRef = useRef(false);[39m
[31m+   const lastPointerMoveAtRef = useRef(0);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => {[39m
[31m+     controlRef.current = control;[39m
[31m+   }, [control]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const send = (payload: unknown) => {[39m
[31m+     if (socketRef.current?.readyState !== WebSocket.OPEN) return false;[39m
[31m+     socketRef.current.send(JSON.stringify(payload));[39m
[31m+     return true;[39m
[31m+   };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const openViewer = async (forceReconnect = false) => {[39m
[31m+     try {[39m
[31m+       socketRef.current?.close(1000, "Replacing CRM viewer session");[39m
[31m+       socketRef.current = null;[39m
[31m+       pendingInputsRef.current = [];[39m
[31m+       pendingNavigationRef.current = null;[39m
[31m+       humanControlRequestedRef.current = false;[39m
[31m+       pendingAiControlRef.current = false;[39m
[31m+       setSession(null);[39m
[31m+       setImage("");[39m
[31m+       setFrameMetadata(null);[39m
[31m+       setStatus("Opening your CRM…");[39m
[31m+       const next = await open.mutateAsync({[39m
[31m+         connectedSystemId,[39m
[31m+         forceReconnect,[39m
[31m+       });[39m
[31m+       setSession(next);[39m
[31m+       setControl(next.control);[39m
[31m+       controlRef.current = next.control;[39m
[31m+       setCurrentUrl(next.url);[39m
[31m+     } catch (error) {[39m
[31m+       setStatus(friendlyError(error, "The CRM workspace could not be opened."));[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+   };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => {[39m
[31m+     void openViewer();[39m
[31m+     return () => socketRef.current?.close();[39m
[31m+     // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps[39m
[31m+   }, [connectedSystemId]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const requestHumanControl = () => {[39m
[31m+     if (controlRef.current === "AGENT_CONTROL") return;[39m
[31m+     if (humanControlRequestedRef.current) return;[39m
[31m+     humanControlRequestedRef.current = true;[39m
[31m+     if (!send({ type: "acquireHumanControl" }))[39m
[31m+       humanControlRequestedRef.current = false;[39m
[31m+   };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const queueOrSendInput = (event: BrowserInputEvent) => {[39m
[31m+     if (controlRef.current === "AGENT_CONTROL") {[39m
[31m+       if (!(event.kind === "mouse" && event.type === "mouseMoved"))[39m
[31m+         toast.info([39m
[31m+           "AmarktAI is working in the CRM. Take control to work manually."[39m
[31m+         );[39m
[31m+       return;[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+     if (controlRef.current === "HUMAN_CONTROL") {[39m
[31m+       send({ type: "input", event });[39m
[31m+       return;[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     if (event.kind === "mouse" && event.type === "mouseMoved") {[39m
[31m+       pendingInputsRef.current = pendingInputsRef.current.filter([39m
[31m+         pending => !(pending.kind === "mouse" && pending.type === "mouseMoved")[39m
[31m+       );[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+     pendingInputsRef.current.push(event);[39m
[31m+     if (pendingInputsRef.current.length > 12)[39m
[31m+       pendingInputsRef.current.splice(0, pendingInputsRef.current.length - 12);[39m
[31m+     requestHumanControl();[39m
[31m+   };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const flushPendingInput = () => {[39m
[31m+     const queued = pendingInputsRef.current.splice(0);[39m
[31m+     for (const event of queued) send({ type: "input", event });[39m
[31m+   };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const flushPendingNavigation = () => {[39m
[31m+     const action = pendingNavigationRef.current;[39m
[31m+     pendingNavigationRef.current = null;[39m
[31m+     if (action) send({ type: "navigation", action });[39m
[31m+   };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const actuallyRequestAiControl = async () => {[39m
[31m+     if (!session) return;[39m
[31m+     pendingAiControlRef.current = false;[39m
[31m+     try {[39m
[31m+       await acquireAi.mutateAsync({ viewerSessionId: session.viewerSessionId });[39m
[31m+     } catch (error) {[39m
[31m+       toast.error([39m
[31m+         friendlyError(error, "AmarktAI could not take CRM control right now.")[39m
[31m+       );[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+   };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => {[39m
[31m+     if (!session) return;[39m
[31m+     const socket = new WebSocket(streamUrl(session));[39m
[31m+     socketRef.current = socket;[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     const sendSize = () => {[39m
[31m+       const rect = viewerRef.current?.getBoundingClientRect();[39m
[31m+       if (!rect || socket.readyState !== WebSocket.OPEN) return;[39m
[31m+       const viewport = crmDesktopViewport({[39m
[31m+         width: rect.width,[39m
[31m+         height: rect.height,[39m
[31m+       });[39m
[31m+       socket.send([39m
[31m+         JSON.stringify({[39m
[31m+           type: "resize",[39m
[31m+           width: viewport.width,[39m
[31m+           height: viewport.height,[39m
[31m+           deviceScaleFactor: 1,[39m
[31m+         })[39m
[31m+       );[39m
[31m+     };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     socket.onopen = () => {[39m
[31m+       setStatus("CRM ready for sign-in");[39m
[31m+       sendSize();[39m
[31m+     };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     socket.onmessage = event => {[39m
[31m+       const message = JSON.parse(String(event.data)) as StreamMessage;[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       if (message.type === "frame") {[39m
[31m+         setImage(`data:image/jpeg;base64,${message.data}`);[39m
[31m+         setFrameMetadata(message.metadata ?? null);[39m
[31m+       } else if (message.type === "control") {[39m
[31m+         setControl(message.control);[39m
[31m+         controlRef.current = message.control;[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+         if (message.control === "HUMAN_CONTROL") {[39m
[31m+           humanControlRequestedRef.current = false;[39m
[31m+           flushPendingInput();[39m
[31m+           flushPendingNavigation();[39m
[31m+         } else if (message.control === "IDLE") {[39m
[31m+           humanControlRequestedRef.current = false;[39m
[31m+           if (pendingAiControlRef.current) void actuallyRequestAiControl();[39m
[31m+           else if ([39m
[31m+             pendingInputsRef.current.length ||[39m
[31m+             pendingNavigationRef.current[39m
[31m+           )[39m
[31m+             requestHumanControl();[39m
[31m+         } else if (message.control === "AGENT_CONTROL") {[39m
[31m+           humanControlRequestedRef.current = false;[39m
[31m+           pendingInputsRef.current = [];[39m
[31m+           pendingNavigationRef.current = null;[39m
[31m+         }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+         setActivity(current =>[39m
[31m+           [[39m
[31m+             `${[39m
[31m+               message.control === "HUMAN_CONTROL"[39m
[31m+                 ? "You took CRM control"[39m
[31m+                 : message.control === "AGENT_CONTROL"[39m
[31m+                   ? "AmarktAI took CRM control"[39m
[31m+                   : "CRM control is idle"[39m
[31m+             } · ${new Date().toLocaleTimeString()}`,[39m
[31m+             ...current,[39m
[31m+           ].slice(0, 8)[39m
[31m+         );[39m
[31m+       } else if (message.type === "ready") {[39m
[31m+         setControl(message.control);[39m
[31m+         controlRef.current = message.control;[39m
[31m+         setCurrentUrl(message.url);[39m
[31m+       } else if (message.type === "navigation") {[39m
[31m+         setCurrentUrl(message.url);[39m
[31m+       } else if (message.type === "session") {[39m
[31m+         setCurrentUrl(message.currentUrl);[39m
[31m+         setAuthenticationState(message.authenticationState);[39m
[31m+         onAuthenticationState(message.authenticationState);[39m
[31m+         setStatus([39m
[31m+           message.errorMessage ||[39m
[31m+             (message.authenticationState === "AUTHENTICATED"[39m
[31m+               ? "Connected"[39m
[31m+               : message.authenticationState === "CHECKING"[39m
[31m+                 ? "Checking your sign-in…"[39m
[31m+                 : message.authenticationState === "MFA_OR_SSO"[39m
[31m+                   ? "Complete verification in the CRM"[39m
[31m+                   : message.authenticationState === "REAUTHENTICATION_REQUIRED"[39m
[31m+                     ? "Sign in again"[39m
[31m+                     : "Sign in directly to your CRM")[39m
[31m+         );[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+         if (message.authenticationState === "AUTHENTICATED")[39m
[31m+           setActivity(current =>[39m
[31m+             current[0]?.startsWith("CRM authenticated")[39m
[31m+               ? current[39m
[31m+               : [[39m
[31m+                   `CRM authenticated · ${new Date().toLocaleTimeString()}`,[39m
[31m+                   ...current,[39m
[31m+                 ].slice(0, 8)[39m
[31m+           );[39m
[31m+       } else if (message.type === "disconnected") {[39m
[31m+         setStatus("The CRM browser connection paused. Reopen it to continue.");[39m
[31m+       } else if (message.type === "error") {[39m
[31m+         const friendly = friendlyError([39m
[31m+           message.message || message.code,[39m
[31m+           "That CRM action could not be completed."[39m
[31m+         );[39m
[31m+         setStatus(friendly);[39m
[31m+         toast.error(friendly);[39m
[31m+       }[39m
[31m+     };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     socket.onclose = event => {[39m
[31m+       setStatus([39m
[31m+         event.code === 4002[39m
[31m+           ? "This CRM was disconnected. Reconnect it from Connections to continue."[39m
[31m+           : "Connection paused. Reconnect to continue."[39m
[31m+       );[39m
[31m+       pendingInputsRef.current = [];[39m
[31m+       pendingNavigationRef.current = null;[39m
[31m+       humanControlRequestedRef.current = false;[39m
[31m+     };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     const observer = new ResizeObserver(sendSize);[39m
[31m+     if (viewerRef.current) observer.observe(viewerRef.current);[39m
[31m+     const heartbeat = window.setInterval(() => {[39m
[31m+       if (socket.readyState === WebSocket.OPEN && !document.hidden)[39m
[31m+         socket.send(JSON.stringify({ type: "ping" }));[39m
[31m+     }, 25_000);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     return () => {[39m
[31m+       window.clearInterval(heartbeat);[39m
[31m+       observer.disconnect();[39m
[31m+       socket.close();[39m
[31m+     };[39m
[31m+     // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps[39m
[31m+   }, [session]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => {[39m
[31m+     const onVisibility = () =>[39m
[31m+       send({ type: "visibility", visible: !document.hidden });[39m
[31m+     document.addEventListener("visibilitychange", onVisibility);[39m
[31m+     onVisibility();[39m
[31m+     return () => document.removeEventListener("visibilitychange", onVisibility);[39m
[31m+     // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps[39m
[31m+   }, [session]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const mapPointer = (clientX: number, clientY: number) => {[39m
[31m+     const rect = viewerRef.current?.getBoundingClientRect();[39m
[31m+     if (!rect) return null;[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     const sourceWidth =[39m
[31m+       frameMetadata?.deviceWidth && frameMetadata.deviceWidth > 0[39m
[31m+         ? frameMetadata.deviceWidth[39m
[31m+         : rect.width;[39m
[31m+     const sourceHeight =[39m
[31m+       frameMetadata?.deviceHeight && frameMetadata.deviceHeight > 0[39m
[31m+         ? frameMetadata.deviceHeight[39m
[31m+         : rect.height;[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     const scale = Math.min([39m
[31m+       rect.width / sourceWidth,[39m
[31m+       rect.height / sourceHeight[39m
[31m+     );[39m
[31m+     const renderedWidth = sourceWidth * scale;[39m
[31m+     const renderedHeight = sourceHeight * scale;[39m
[31m+     const offsetX = (rect.width - renderedWidth) / 2;[39m
[31m+     const offsetY = (rect.height - renderedHeight) / 2;[39m
[31m+     const localX = clientX - rect.left - offsetX;[39m
[31m+     const localY = clientY - rect.top - offsetY;[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     if ([39m
[31m+       localX < 0 ||[39m
[31m+       localY < 0 ||[39m
[31m+       localX > renderedWidth ||[39m
[31m+       localY > renderedHeight[39m
[31m+     )[39m
[31m+       return null;[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     return {[39m
[31m+       x: localX / scale,[39m
[31m+       y: localY / scale,[39m
[31m+     };[39m
[31m+   };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const forwardPointer = ([39m
[31m+     event: PointerEvent<HTMLDivElement>,[39m
[31m+     type: "mousePressed" | "mouseReleased" | "mouseMoved"[39m
[31m+   ) => {[39m
[31m+     if (type === "mouseMoved") {[39m
[31m+       if (controlRef.current === "AGENT_CONTROL") return;[39m
[31m+       const now = performance.now();[39m
[31m+       if (now - lastPointerMoveAtRef.current < 30) return;[39m
[31m+       lastPointerMoveAtRef.current = now;[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+     const point = mapPointer(event.clientX, event.clientY);[39m
[31m+     if (!point) return;[39m
[31m+     if (type === "mousePressed")[39m
[31m+       viewerRef.current?.focus({ preventScroll: true });[39m
[31m+     queueOrSendInput({[39m
[31m+       kind: "mouse",[39m
[31m+       type,[39m
[31m+       ...point,[39m
[31m+       button:[39m
[31m+         event.button === 2[39m
[31m+           ? "right"[39m
[31m+           : event.button === 1[39m
[31m+             ? "middle"[39m
[31m+             : event.button === 0[39m
[31m+               ? "left"[39m
[31m+               : "none",[39m
[31m+       clickCount: Math.max(1, event.detail || 1),[39m
[31m+     });[39m
[31m+   };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const forwardWheel = (event: WheelEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {[39m
[31m+     event.preventDefault();[39m
[31m+     const point = mapPointer(event.clientX, event.clientY);[39m
[31m+     const rect = viewerRef.current?.getBoundingClientRect();[39m
[31m+     if (!point || !rect) return;[39m
[31m+     queueOrSendInput({[39m
[31m+       kind: "mouse",[39m
[31m+       type: "mouseWheel",[39m
[31m+       ...point,[39m
[31m+       deltaX: normalizeCrmWheelDelta(event.deltaX, event.deltaMode, rect.width),[39m
[31m+       deltaY: normalizeCrmWheelDelta([39m
[31m+         event.deltaY,[39m
[31m+         event.deltaMode,[39m
[31m+         rect.height[39m
[31m+       ),[39m
[31m+     });[39m
[31m+   };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const forwardKey = ([39m
[31m+     event: KeyboardEvent<HTMLDivElement>,[39m
[31m+     type: "keyDown" | "keyUp"[39m
[31m+   ) => {[39m
[31m+     const commandKey = event.metaKey || event.ctrlKey;[39m
[31m+     const key = event.key.toLowerCase();[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     // Keep browser/app-level shortcuts outside the remote page. Paste is the[39m
[31m+     // exception: allow the browser to emit the ClipboardEvent, which is then[39m
[31m+     // forwarded through the bounded Input.insertText path below.[39m
[31m+     if (commandKey && ["l", "r", "t", "n", "q", "w"].includes(key)) return;[39m
[31m+     if (commandKey && key === "v") return;[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     event.preventDefault();[39m
[31m+     const modifiers =[39m
[31m+       (event.altKey ? 1 : 0) |[39m
[31m+       (event.ctrlKey ? 2 : 0) |[39m
[31m+       (event.metaKey ? 4 : 0) |[39m
[31m+       (event.shiftKey ? 8 : 0);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     queueOrSendInput({[39m
[31m+       kind: "key",[39m
[31m+       type,[39m
[31m+       key: event.key,[39m
[31m+       code: event.code,[39m
[31m+       text:[39m
[31m+         type === "keyDown" &&[39m
[31m+         event.key.length === 1 &&[39m
[31m+         !event.ctrlKey &&[39m
[31m+         !event.metaKey[39m
[31m+           ? event.key[39m
[31m+           : "",[39m
[31m+       modifiers,[39m
[31m+     });[39m
[31m+   };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const forwardPaste = (event: ClipboardEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {[39m
[31m+     event.preventDefault();[39m
[31m+     const text = event.clipboardData.getData("text").slice(0, 4_000);[39m
[31m+     if (!text) return;[39m
[31m+     queueOrSendInput({[39m
[31m+       kind: "key",[39m
[31m+       type: "keyDown",[39m
[31m+       key: "",[39m
[31m+       code: "",[39m
[31m+       text,[39m
[31m+       modifiers: 0,[39m
[31m+     });[39m
[31m+   };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const takeControl = async () => {[39m
[31m+     try {[39m
[31m+       if (controlRef.current === "AGENT_CONTROL" && session) {[39m
[31m+         await releaseAi.mutateAsync({[39m
[31m+           viewerSessionId: session.viewerSessionId,[39m
[31m+         });[39m
[31m+         // The mutation has synchronously released the server arbitration lock.[39m
[31m+         // Do not wait on a websocket IDLE frame before asking for human control.[39m
[31m+         controlRef.current = "IDLE";[39m
[31m+         setControl("IDLE");[39m
[31m+         humanControlRequestedRef.current = false;[39m
[31m+       }[39m
[31m+       requestHumanControl();[39m
[31m+       viewerRef.current?.focus({ preventScroll: true });[39m
[31m+     } catch (error) {[39m
[31m+       toast.error([39m
[31m+         friendlyError(error, "CRM control could not be returned to you.")[39m
[31m+       );[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+   };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const requestAi = () => {[39m
[31m+     if (!session || controlRef.current === "AGENT_CONTROL") return;[39m
[31m+     pendingAiControlRef.current = true;[39m
[31m+     pendingNavigationRef.current = null;[39m
[31m+     if (controlRef.current === "HUMAN_CONTROL")[39m
[31m+       send({ type: "releaseHumanControl" });[39m
[31m+     else void actuallyRequestAiControl();[39m
[31m+   };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const navigateBrowser = (action: BrowserNavigationAction) => {[39m
[31m+     if (controlRef.current === "AGENT_CONTROL") {[39m
[31m+       toast.info([39m
[31m+         "AmarktAI is working in the CRM. Take control before navigating."[39m
[31m+       );[39m
[31m+       return;[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+     if (controlRef.current === "HUMAN_CONTROL") {[39m
[31m+       send({ type: "navigation", action });[39m
[31m+       return;[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+     pendingNavigationRef.current = action;[39m
[31m+     requestHumanControl();[39m
[31m+   };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const confirmSignedIn = () => send({ type: "customerFinishedSigningIn" });[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const submitAssistant = async () => {[39m
[31m+     if (!session || !assistantPrompt.trim()) return;[39m
[31m+     try {[39m
[31m+       setAssistantWorkflowRunId(null);[39m
[31m+       const result = await askAssistant.mutateAsync({[39m
[31m+         viewerSessionId: session.viewerSessionId,[39m
[31m+         command: assistantPrompt.trim(),[39m
[31m+       });[39m
[31m+       setAssistantResult(result.summary);[39m
[31m+       setAssistantWorkflowRunId([39m
[31m+         typeof result.workflowRunId === "number" ? result.workflowRunId : null[39m
[31m+       );[39m
[31m+       setAssistantPrompt("");[39m
[31m+     } catch (error) {[39m
[31m+       setAssistantWorkflowRunId(null);[39m
[31m+       setAssistantResult([39m
[31m+         friendlyError(error, "AmarktAI could not complete that request.")[39m
[31m+       );[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+   };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const domain = (() => {[39m
[31m+     try {[39m
[31m+       return new URL(currentUrl).hostname;[39m
[31m+     } catch {[39m
[31m+       return crmName;[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+   })();[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const authReady = authenticationState === "AUTHENTICATED";[39m
[31m+   const availableSystems = systems.filter(system => system.baseUrl);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   return ([39m
[31m+     <div className="flex h-full min-h-0 flex-col bg-white">[39m
[31m+       <header className="flex h-12 shrink-0 items-center gap-2 border-b border-[#D7E0EA] bg-[#F7F9FC] px-2.5">[39m
[31m+         <Button[39m
[31m+           variant="ghost"[39m
[31m+           size="sm"[39m
[31m+           className="h-8 px-2 text-[#526277]"[39m
[31m+           onClick={onToday}[39m
[31m+         >[39m
[31m+           <ChevronLeft className="mr-1 h-4 w-4" />[39m
[31m+           <span className="hidden xl:inline">Today</span>[39m

[31m+[39m
[31m+         <div className="h-6 w-px bg-[#D7E0EA]" />[39m

[31m+           >[39m
[31m+             <ArrowLeft className="h-4 w-4" />[39m
[31m+           </Button>[39m

[31m+             aria-label="Forward"[39m
[31m+             variant="ghost"[39m

[31m+             <RefreshCw className="h-4 w-4" />[39m
[31m+           </Button>[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+         <div className="flex min-w-0 flex-1 items-center gap-2 rounded-lg border border-[#D7E0EA] bg-white px-3 py-1.5 text-xs shadow-sm">[39m

[31m+               aria-label="Choose CRM"[39m

[31m+         </div>[39m

[31m+               authReady ? "bg-emerald-500" : "bg-amber-400"[39m
[31m+             }`}[39m
[31m+           />[39m
[31m+           <span className="max-w-52 truncate text-xs font-semibold text-[#526277]">[39m
[31m+             {status}[39m
[31m+           </span>[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+[39m

[31m+           variant={assistantOpen ? "default" : "outline"}[39m
[31m+           size="sm"[39m
[31m+           className="h-8"[39m
[31m+           onClick={() => setAssistantOpen(open => !open)}[39m
[31m+         >[39m
[31m+           <Sparkles className="mr-1.5 h-4 w-4" />[39m
[31m+           Assistant[39m
[31m+         </Button>[39m
[31m+       </header>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       <div className="relative flex min-h-0 flex-1">[39m
[31m+         <section className="relative min-w-0 flex-1 bg-[#E9EEF5]">[39m
[31m+           <div[39m
[31m+             ref={viewerRef}[39m
[31m+             role="application"[39m
[31m+             aria-label={`${crmName} live CRM`}[39m
[31m+             tabIndex={0}[39m
[31m+             onPointerEnter={() => requestHumanControl()}[39m
[31m+             onPointerDown={event => forwardPointer(event, "mousePressed")}[39m
[31m+             onPointerUp={event => forwardPointer(event, "mouseReleased")}[39m
[31m+             onPointerMove={event => forwardPointer(event, "mouseMoved")}[39m
[31m+             onWheel={forwardWheel}[39m
[31m+             onKeyDown={event => forwardKey(event, "keyDown")}[39m
[31m+             onKeyUp={event => forwardKey(event, "keyUp")}[39m
[31m+             onPaste={forwardPaste}[39m
[31m+             onContextMenu={event => event.preventDefault()}[39m
[31m+             className="relative h-full w-full cursor-default overflow-hidden bg-[#E9EEF5] outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-inset focus-visible:ring-[#3F70D8]"[39m
[31m+           >[39m
[31m+             {image ? ([39m
[31m+               <img[39m
[31m+                 src={image}[39m
[31m+                 alt=""[39m
[31m+                 aria-hidden="true"[39m
[31m+                 draggable={false}[39m
[31m+                 className="pointer-events-none h-full w-full select-none object-contain"[39m
[31m+               />[39m
[31m+             ) : ([39m
[31m+               <div className="flex h-full flex-col items-center justify-center gap-3 text-[#6C798B]">[39m
[31m+                 <Loader2 className="h-7 w-7 animate-spin text-[#3F70D8]" />[39m
[31m+                 <p className="text-sm font-semibold">{status}</p>[39m
[31m+               </div>[39m
[31m+             )}[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+             <div className="pointer-events-none absolute bottom-3 left-3 flex items-center gap-2 rounded-full border border-white/60 bg-[#26354A]/90 px-3 py-1.5 text-[11px] font-bold text-white shadow-md backdrop-blur">[39m
[31m+               <MousePointer2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />[39m
[31m+               {control === "HUMAN_CONTROL"[39m
[31m+                 ? "You control the CRM"[39m
[31m+                 : control === "AGENT_CONTROL"[39m
[31m+                   ? "AmarktAI is working"[39m
[31m+                   : "Move here to take control"}[39m
[31m+             </div>[39m
[31m+           </div>[39m
[31m+         </section>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+         {assistantOpen ? ([39m
[31m+           <aside className="absolute inset-y-0 right-0 z-30 flex w-[min(390px,94vw)] flex-col border-l border-[#D7E0EA] bg-white shadow-[-18px_0_45px_rgba(38,53,74,.12)] lg:relative lg:z-auto lg:w-[370px] lg:shadow-none">[39m
[31m+             <div className="flex h-12 shrink-0 items-center justify-between border-b border-[#D7E0EA] px-4">[39m

[31m+                     Ready[39m
[31m+                   </p>[39m
[31m+                 </div>[39m

[31m+                 variant="ghost"[39m

[31m+                 <p className="text-xs font-bold text-[#26354A]">{status}</p>[39m
[31m+                 <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-5 text-[#6C798B]">[39m
[31m+                   {authReady[39m
[31m+                     ? "Your private CRM session is connected. Ask me to work with the customer or use the CRM yourself."[39m
[31m+                     : "Finish signing in inside the CRM. Your password and verification code stay between you and the CRM."}[39m
[31m+                 </p>[39m
[31m+               </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+               {assistantResult ? ([39m
[31m+                 <div className="mt-3 rounded-xl border border-[#D7E0EA] bg-white p-3 text-sm leading-6 text-[#33445B]">[39m
[31m+                   {assistantResult}[39m

[31m+                 <summary className="cursor-pointer list-none px-3 py-2.5 text-xs font-bold text-[#33445B]">[39m
[31m+                   CRM controls[39m
[31m+                 </summary>[39m
[31m+                 <div className="space-y-2 border-t border-[#E5EAF0] p-3">[39m
[31m+                   <Button[39m
[31m+                     variant="outline"[39m
[31m+                     size="sm"[39m
[31m+                     className="w-full justify-start"[39m
[31m+                     onClick={() => void takeControl()}[39m
[31m+                   >[39m
[31m+                     <MousePointer2 className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" />[39m
[31m+                     {control === "HUMAN_CONTROL"[39m
[31m+                       ? "You have control"[39m
[31m+                       : "Take control"}[39m
[31m+                   </Button>[39m
[31m+                   <Button[39m
[31m+                     variant="outline"[39m
[31m+                     size="sm"[39m
[31m+                     className="w-full justify-start"[39m
[31m+                     onClick={requestAi}[39m
[31m+                     disabled={[39m
[31m+                       control === "AGENT_CONTROL" || acquireAi.isPending[39m
[31m+                     }[39m
[31m+                   >[39m
[31m+                     <ShieldCheck className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" />[39m
[31m+                     {control === "AGENT_CONTROL"[39m
[31m+                       ? "AmarktAI has control"[39m

[31m+                   </Button>[39m
[31m+                   {!authReady ? ([39m
[31m+                     <Button[39m
[31m+                       size="sm"[39m
[31m+                       className="w-full justify-start"[39m
[31m+                       onClick={confirmSignedIn}[39m
[31m+                     >[39m
[31m+                       Check my sign-in[39m
[31m+                     </Button>[39m
[31m+                   ) : null}[39m

[31m+                     variant="ghost"[39m
[31m+                     size="sm"[39m
[31m+                     className="w-full justify-start"[39m
[31m+                     onClick={() => void openViewer(true)}[39m
[31m+                     disabled={open.isPending}[39m
[31m+                   >[39m
[31m+                     <RefreshCw[39m
[31m+                       className={`mr-2 h-4 w-4 ${[39m
[31m+                         open.isPending ? "animate-spin" : ""[39m
[31m+                       }`}[39m
[31m+                     />[39m
[31m+                     Reconnect browser[39m
[31m+                   </Button>[39m
[31m+                 </div>[39m
[31m+               </details>[39m

[31m+ function pageTitle(location: string) {[39m
[31m+   if (location.startsWith("/customers")) return "Customers";[39m
[31m+   if (location.startsWith("/calls")) return "Calls";[39m

[31m+   if (location.startsWith("/reviews")) return "Review";[39m

[31m+ function SecondFactorGate({[39m
[31m+   hasEmail,[39m
[31m+   smtpReady,[39m
[31m+ }: {[39m
[31m+   hasEmail: boolean;[39m
[31m+   smtpReady: boolean;[39m
[31m+ }) {[39m
[31m+   const [requested, setRequested] = useState(false);[39m
[31m+   const [code, setCode] = useState("");[39m
[31m+   const [feedback, setFeedback] = useState("");[39m
[31m+   const status = trpc.security.status.useQuery();[39m
[31m+   const requestCode = trpc.security.requestEmailCode.useMutation({[39m
[31m+     onSuccess: () => {[39m
[31m+       setRequested(true);[39m
[31m+       setFeedback("");[39m
[31m+       toast.success("Verification code sent.");[39m
[31m+     },[39m
[31m+     onError: () => setFeedback("The code could not be sent. Please try again."),[39m
[31m+   });[39m
[31m+   const verifyCode = trpc.security.verifyEmailCode.useMutation({[39m
[31m+     onSuccess: () => {[39m
[31m+       setFeedback("");[39m
[31m+       status.refetch();[39m
[31m+       window.location.assign("/dashboard");[39m
[31m+     },[39m
[31m+     onError: () =>[39m
[31m+       setFeedback([39m
[31m+         "That code wasn’t accepted. Check the six digits or request a new one."[39m
[31m+       ),[39m
[31m+   });[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   return ([39m
[31m+     <div className="grid min-h-screen place-items-center bg-[#F5F7FA] p-5 text-[#26354A]">[39m
[31m+       <div className="w-full max-w-lg rounded-2xl border border-[#DCE2E9] bg-white p-7 shadow-sm sm:p-8">[39m
[31m+         <BrandMark />[39m
[31m+         <div className="mt-8 flex items-center gap-3">[39m
[31m+           <span className="grid size-10 place-items-center rounded-xl bg-[#EDF3FF] text-[#315BB6]">[39m
[31m+             <LockKeyhole size={19} />[39m
[31m+           </span>[39m
[31m+           <div>[39m
[31m+             <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-[.12em] text-[#6C798B]">[39m
[31m+               Security check[39m
[31m+             </p>[39m
[31m+             <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold">Confirm access</h1>[39m
[31m+           </div>[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+         {feedback ? ([39m
[31m+           <p[39m
[31m+             role="alert"[39m
[31m+             className="mt-5 rounded-xl border border-red-300 bg-red-50 p-4 text-sm font-semibold text-red-900"[39m
[31m+           >[39m
[31m+             {feedback}[39m
[31m+           </p>[39m
[31m+         ) : null}[39m
[31m+         {!hasEmail ? ([39m
[31m+           <Notice text="This account needs an email address before access can be verified." />[39m
[31m+         ) : !smtpReady ? ([39m
[31m+           <Notice text="Email verification is not available yet. Ask the administrator to finish email setup." />[39m
[31m+         ) : !requested ? ([39m
[31m+           <>[39m
[31m+             <p className="mt-4 text-sm leading-6 text-[#6C798B]">[39m
[31m+               We’ll send a six-digit code to your account email.[39m
[31m+             </p>[39m
[31m+             <Button[39m
[31m+               onClick={() => requestCode.mutate()}[39m
[31m+               disabled={requestCode.isPending}[39m
[31m+               className="mt-5 h-12 w-full"[39m
[31m+             >[39m
[31m+               <MailCheck className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" />[39m
[31m+               {requestCode.isPending ? "Sending…" : "Send code"}[39m
[31m+             </Button>[39m
[31m+           </>[39m
[31m+         ) : ([39m
[31m+           <>[39m
[31m+             <p className="mt-4 text-sm leading-6 text-[#6C798B]">[39m
[31m+               Enter the six-digit code.[39m
[31m+             </p>[39m
[31m+             <input[39m
[31m+               inputMode="numeric"[39m
[31m+               maxLength={6}[39m
[31m+               value={code}[39m
[31m+               onChange={event => setCode(event.target.value.replace(/\D/g, ""))}[39m
[31m+               placeholder="000000"[39m
[31m+               className="mt-5 h-14 w-full rounded-xl border-2 border-[#D5DDE7] text-center text-2xl font-bold tracking-[.3em] outline-none focus:border-[#6F91E2]"[39m
[31m+             />[39m
[31m+             <Button[39m
[31m+               onClick={() => verifyCode.mutate({ code })}[39m
[31m+               disabled={code.length !== 6 || verifyCode.isPending}[39m
[31m+               className="mt-4 h-12 w-full"[39m
[31m+             >[39m
[31m+               {verifyCode.isPending ? "Checking…" : "Continue"}[39m
[31m+             </Button>[39m
[31m+             <button[39m
[31m+               onClick={() => requestCode.mutate()}[39m
[31m+               className="mt-4 w-full text-sm font-bold text-[#3F70D8]"[39m
[31m+             >[39m
[31m+               Send a new code[39m
[31m+             </button>[39m
[31m+           </>[39m
[31m+         )}[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+     </div>[39m
[31m+   );[39m
[31m+ }[39m

function statusPresentation(status: string) {
  if (status === \"ready\")
    return {
      tone: \"ready\" as const,
      title: \"Connected\",
      detail: \"The connection is available and can feed the AmarktAI workspace.\",
    };
  if (status === \"testing\" || status === \"connecting\")
    return {
      tone: \"working\" as const,
      title: \"Commissioning\",
      detail: \"AmarktAI is safely checking which CRM data and operations are available.\",
    };
  if (status === \"authentication_expired\")
    return {
      tone: \"attention\" as const,
      title: \"Sign in again\",
      detail: \"The saved CRM session expired. Reopen the source CRM and sign in directly.\",
    };
  if (status === \"limited_permissions\")
    return {
      tone: \"attention\" as const,
      title: \"Connected with limits\",
      detail: \"The CRM is connected, but some operations still need permission or commissioning.\",
    };
  if (status === \"needs_attention\" || status === \"error\")
    return {
      tone: \"attention\" as const,
      title: \"Needs attention\",
      detail: \"Open the source CRM to finish the connection or resolve the sign-in issue.\",
    };
  return {
    tone: \"working\" as const,
    title: \"Ready to sign in\",
    detail: \"Open the source CRM and sign in with your own account to continue.\",
  };
}

async function responseError(response: Response) {
  try {
    const body = (await response.json()) as { error?: string };
    return body.error || `Request failed (${response.status})`;
  } catch {
    return `Request failed (${response.status})`;
  }
}

export default function ConnectionsV2() {
  const [, navigate] = useLocation();
  const organisation = trpc.organisation.current.useQuery();
  const organisationId = organisation.data?.organisationId;
  const systems = trpc.connectedSystems.list.useQuery(
    { organisationId: organisationId || 0 },
    { enabled: Boolean(organisationId), refetchInterval: 5_000 }
  );
  const readiness = trpc.integrations.list.useQuery(undefined, {
    enabled: Boolean(organisationId),
    retry: false,
  });
  const create = trpc.connectedSystems.create.useMutation();
  const beginOAuth = trpc.connectedSystems.beginOAuth.useMutation();
  const [adding, setAdding] = useState(false);
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<(typeof providers)[number]>(
    providers[0]
  );
  const [customUrl, setCustomUrl] = useState(\"\");
  const [error, setError] = useState(\"\");
  const [disconnectingId, setDisconnectingId] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [confirmDisconnectId, setConfirmDisconnectId] = useState<number | null>(
    null
  );
  const canManage =
    organisation.data?.role === \"owner\" ||
    organisation.data?.role === \"manager\";
  const startUrl =
    selected.provider === \"custom_browser\" ? customUrl.trim() : selected.url;
  const connected = useMemo(() => systems.data || [], [systems.data]);
  const workspaceReady = Boolean(readiness.data?.genie.ready);

  async function connect() {
    if (!organisationId) return;
    setError(\"\");
    try {
      const parsed = new URL(startUrl);
      if (parsed.protocol !== \"https:\") throw new Error(\"https required\");
      const id = await create.mutateAsync({
        organisationId,
        provider: selected.provider,
        displayName: selected.label,
        baseUrl: startUrl,
        connectionMethod: selected.transport,
        allowedReadCapabilities: readCapabilities,
        allowedWriteCapabilities: writeCapabilities,
      });
      await systems.refetch();
      setAdding(false);
      if (selected.transport === \"oauth\") {
        const result = await beginOAuth.mutateAsync({
          organisationId,
          connectedSystemId: id,
        });
        window.location.assign(result.authorizationUrl);
      } else {
        toast.success(\"CRM added. Sign in inside the secure source workspace to continue commissioning.\");
        navigate(`/crm/${id}`);
      }
    } catch (cause) {
      setError(
        friendlyError(
          cause,
          \"The CRM connection could not be changed safely. Try again.\"
        )
      );
    }
  }

  async function disconnect(connectedSystemId: number) {
    if (!canManage) return;
    if (confirmDisconnectId !== connectedSystemId) {
      setConfirmDisconnectId(connectedSystemId);
      return;
    }
    setDisconnectingId(connectedSystemId);
    setError(\"\");
    try {
      const response = await fetch(
        `/api/connected-system-admin/${connectedSystemId}/disconnect`,
        {
          method: \"POST\",
          credentials: \"include\",
          headers: { \"Content-Type\": \"application/json\" },
          body: \"{}\",
        }
      );
      if (!response.ok) throw new Error(await responseError(response));
      setConfirmDisconnectId(null);
      toast.success(
        \"CRM disconnected. Sign-in data was removed while retained CRM history and audit evidence were preserved.\"
      );
      await systems.refetch();
    } catch (cause) {
      const message = friendlyError(
        cause,
        \"The CRM connection could not be changed safely. Try again.\"
      );
      setError(message);
      toast.error(message);
    } finally {
      setDisconnectingId(null);
    }
  }

  return (
    <DashboardLayout>
      <div className=\"mx-auto max-w-[1320px] space-y-5 text-[#26354A]\">
        <header className=\"rounded-3xl border border-[#DCE4EE] bg-white p-6 shadow-sm sm:p-8\">
          <div className=\"flex flex-col gap-5 lg:flex-row lg:items-end lg:justify-between\">
            <div>
              <p className=\"handover-kicker\">CRM setup</p>
              <h1 className=\"mt-3 font-display text-4xl font-bold tracking-[-.06em] sm:text-5xl\">
                Connect the CRM once. Work from AmarktAI every day.
              </h1>
              <p className=\"mt-3 max-w-3xl text-sm leading-6 text-[#66758A] sm:text-base\">
                The CRM stays the system of record. AmarktAI brings customer, opportunity, task, activity and sales context into a cleaner workspace so salespeople can focus on calls, follow-up and decisions instead of CRM navigation.
              </p>
            </div>
            {canManage ? (
              <Button onClick={() => setAdding(value => !value)}>
                {adding ? (
                  <X className=\"mr-2 h-4 w-4\" />
                ) : (
                  <Plus className=\"mr-2 h-4 w-4\" />
                )}
                {adding ? \"Close\" : \"Connect CRM\"}
              </Button>
            ) : null}
          </div>
        </header>

        <section className=\"grid gap-3 md:grid-cols-3\">
          <JourneyStep
            number=\"1\"
            title=\"Connect\"
            detail=\"Manager connects the company CRM and each salesperson signs in with their own account where required.\"
          />
          <JourneyStep
            number=\"2\"
            title=\"Prove\"
            detail=\"AmarktAI commissions the required read and write operations before the workspace can become Ready.\"
          />
          <JourneyStep
            number=\"3\"
            title=\"Work here\"
            detail=\"Customers, calls, tasks, opportunities, notes and approved actions move into the AmarktAI workflow.\"
          />
        </section>

        <section className={`rounded-2xl border p-4 ${workspaceReady ? \"border-emerald-200 bg-emerald-50\" : \"border-[#D8E2F0] bg-[#F6F9FD]\"}`}>
          <div className=\"flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3\">
            <div className=\"flex items-start gap-3\">
              <span className={`grid h-10 w-10 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-xl ${workspaceReady ? \"bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700\" : \"bg-[#EAF1FF] text-[#2F6FED]\"}`}>
                {workspaceReady ? <CheckCircle2 className=\"h-5 w-5\" /> : <Sparkles className=\"h-5 w-5\" />}
              </span>
              <div>
                <p className=\"font-bold text-[#26354A]\">
                  {workspaceReady ? \"Core CRM workspace ready\" : \"CRM commissioning still in progress\"}
                </p>
                <p className=\"mt-1 text-sm leading-5 text-[#66758A]\">
                  {workspaceReady
                    ? \"The required CRM operations are proven for the AmarktAI workspace.\"
                    : \"A connection alone is not enough. Required operations must be proven before setup is considered complete.\"}
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>
            {!workspaceReady && connected.length ? (
              <span className=\"text-xs font-bold text-[#55708F]\">
                Use Teach AmarktAI below if a required function needs help.
              </span>
            ) : null}
          </div>
        </section>

        {adding ? (
          <section className=\"handover-surface p-5 sm:p-6\">
            <div className=\"flex items-center gap-3\">
              <span className=\"grid h-10 w-10 place-items-center rounded-xl bg-[#EDF3FF] text-[#2F6FED]\">
                <DatabaseZap className=\"h-5 w-5\" />
              </span>
              <div>
                <h2 className=\"font-display text-2xl font-bold tracking-[-.035em]\">
                  Choose the company CRM
                </h2>
                <p className=\"mt-1 text-sm text-[#718096]\">
                  Provider names appear only here because this is the technical connection step.
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className=\"mt-5 grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3\">
              {providers.map(item => (
                <button
                  key={item.provider}
                  type=\"button\"
                  onClick={() => {
                    setSelected(item);
                    setError(\"\");
                  }}
                  className={`rounded-2xl border p-4 text-left transition ${
                    selected.provider === item.provider
                      ? \"border-[#2F6FED] bg-[#F1F6FF] shadow-sm\"
                      : \"border-[#DCE4EE] bg-white hover:border-[#AFC3E8] hover:bg-[#FAFCFF]\"
                  }`}
                >
                  <span className=\"font-bold text-[#26354A]\">{item.label}</span>
                  <span className=\"mt-1 block text-xs text-[#718096]\">
                    {item.transport === \"oauth\"
                      ? \"Secure account connection\"
                      : \"Private browser connection\"}
                  </span>
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>

            {selected.provider === \"custom_browser\" ? (
              <label className=\"mt-5 block max-w-xl text-sm font-bold text-[#33445B]\">
                CRM sign-in address
                <input
                  value={customUrl}
                  onChange={event => {
                    setCustomUrl(event.target.value);
                    setError(\"\");
                  }}
                  placeholder=\"https://crm.example.com/\"
                  className=\"mt-2 h-11 w-full rounded-xl border border-[#C9D4E2] bg-white px-3 text-[#26354A] outline-none focus:border-[#2F6FED] focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#DCE7F6]\"
                />
              </label>
            ) : null}

            {error ? (
              <p
                role=\"alert\"
                className=\"mt-4 flex max-w-2xl items-start gap-2 rounded-xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50 px-3 py-3 text-sm text-red-800\"
              >
                <AlertCircle className=\"mt-0.5 h-4 w-4 shrink-0\" />
                {error}
              </p>
            ) : null}

            <Button
              className=\"mt-5\"
              onClick={() => void connect()}
              disabled={!startUrl || create.isPending}
            >
              {create.isPending ? (
                <Loader2 className=\"mr-2 h-4 w-4 animate-spin\" />
              ) : (
                <ExternalLink className=\"mr-2 h-4 w-4\" />
              )}
              {selected.transport === \"browser\"
                ? \"Open secure sign-in\"
                : \"Connect account\"}
            </Button>
          </section>
        ) : null}

        {!adding && error ? (
          <p
            role=\"alert\"
            className=\"flex items-start gap-2 rounded-xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-800\"
          >
            <AlertCircle className=\"mt-0.5 h-4 w-4 shrink-0\" />
            {error}
          </p>
        ) : null}

        <section className=\"grid gap-4\">
          {systems.isLoading ? (
            <div className=\"handover-surface p-8 text-[#66758A]\">
              <Loader2 className=\"mr-2 inline h-4 w-4 animate-spin\" />
              Loading CRM connections…
            </div>
          ) : null}

          {connected.map(system => {
            const presentation = statusPresentation(system.status);
            const confirming = confirmDisconnectId === system.id;

            return (
              <article key={system.id} className=\"rounded-2xl border border-[#DCE4EE] bg-white p-5 shadow-sm\">
                <div className=\"flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-4\">
                  <div className=\"min-w-0 flex-1\">
                    <div className=\"flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2\">
                      {presentation.tone === \"ready\" ? (
                        <CheckCircle2 className=\"h-5 w-5 shrink-0 text-emerald-600\" />
                      ) : presentation.tone === \"attention\" ? (
                        <AlertCircle className=\"h-5 w-5 shrink-0 text-amber-600\" />
                      ) : (
                        <Loader2 className=\"h-5 w-5 shrink-0 text-[#2F6FED]\" />
                      )}
                      <h2 className=\"truncate text-lg font-bold text-[#26354A]\">
                        {system.displayName}
                      </h2>
                      <span
                        className={`rounded-full px-2.5 py-1 text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-[.06em] ${
                          presentation.tone === \"ready\"
                            ? \"bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700\"
                            : presentation.tone === \"attention\"
                              ? \"bg-amber-50 text-amber-700\"
                              : \"bg-blue-50 text-blue-700\"
                        }`}
                      >
                        {presentation.title}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                    <p className=\"mt-2 max-w-3xl text-sm leading-6 text-[#66758A]\">
                      {presentation.detail}
                    </p>
                  </div>

                  <div className=\"flex flex-wrap gap-2\">
                    <Button
                      variant=\"outline\"
                      onClick={() => navigate(`/crm/${system.id}`)}
                    >
                      <ShieldCheck className=\"mr-2 h-4 w-4\" />
                      {system.status === \"authentication_expired\"
                        ? \"Sign in again\"
                        : \"Open source CRM\"}
                    </Button>
                    <Button
                      variant=\"ghost\"
                      aria-label=\"Refresh connection status\"
                      onClick={() => void systems.refetch()}
                    >
                      <RefreshCw className=\"h-4 w-4\" />
                    </Button>
                  </div>
                </div>

                {canManage ? (
                  <div className=\"mt-4 border-t border-[#E5EAF0] pt-4\">
                    {confirming ? (
                      <div className=\"flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 rounded-xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50 p-3\">
                        <div>
                          <p className=\"text-sm font-bold text-red-800\">
                            Disconnect this CRM?
                          </p>
                          <p className=\"mt-1 text-xs leading-5 text-red-700\">
                            Saved authentication is removed. Retained CRM history and audit evidence remain available.
                          </p>
                        </div>
                        <div className=\"flex gap-2\">
                          <Button
                            variant=\"ghost\"
                            size=\"sm\"
                            onClick={() => setConfirmDisconnectId(null)}
                            disabled={disconnectingId === system.id}
                          >
                            Keep connected
                          </Button>
                          <Button
                            size=\"sm\"
                            className=\"bg-red-600 text-white hover:bg-red-700\"
                            onClick={() => void disconnect(system.id)}
                            disabled={disconnectingId === system.id}
                          >
                            {disconnectingId === system.id ? (
                              <Loader2 className=\"mr-2 h-4 w-4 animate-spin\" />
                            ) : (
                              <Trash2 className=\"mr-2 h-4 w-4\" />
                            )}
                            Disconnect
                          </Button>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    ) : (
                      <button
                        type=\"button\"
                        className=\"text-xs font-semibold text-[#8793A4] transition hover:text-red-700\"
                        onClick={() => setConfirmDisconnectId(system.id)}
                      >
                        Disconnect CRM
                      </button>
                    )}
                  </div>
                ) : null}
              </article>
            );
          })}

          {!systems.isLoading && connected.length === 0 ? (
            <div className=\"rounded-3xl border border-dashed border-[#C9D4E2] bg-white p-12 text-center shadow-sm\">
              <DatabaseZap className=\"mx-auto h-9 w-9 text-[#2F6FED]\" />
              <h2 className=\"mt-4 font-display text-2xl font-bold tracking-[-.035em]\">
                No CRM connected yet
              </h2>
              <p className=\"mx-auto mt-2 max-w-xl text-sm leading-6 text-[#718096]\">
                Connect the company CRM once. After commissioning, customer and sales activity will flow into AmarktAI automatically.
              </p>
              {canManage ? (
                <Button className=\"mt-5\" onClick={() => setAdding(true)}>
                  <Plus className=\"mr-2 h-4 w-4\" /> Connect CRM
                </Button>
              ) : null}
            </div>
          ) : null}
        </section>
      </div>
    </DashboardLayout>
  );
}

function JourneyStep({
  number,
  title,
  detail,
}: {
  number: string;
  title: string;
  detail: string;
}) {
  return (
    <div className=\"handover-surface flex items-start gap-4 p-4\">
      <span className=\"grid h-9 w-9 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-xl bg-[#EDF3FF] text-sm font-black text-[#2F6FED]\">
        {number}
      </span>
      <div>
        <p className=\"font-bold text-[#26354A]\">{title}</p>
        <p className=\"mt-1 text-xs leading-5 text-[#718096]\">{detail}</p>
      </div>
      <ArrowRight className=\"ml-auto mt-2 hidden h-4 w-4 text-[#B1BDCC] md:block\" />
    </div>
  );
}

[31m+ function streamUrl(session: ViewerReady) {[39m
[31m+   const protocol = window.location.protocol === "https:" ? "wss:" : "ws:";[39m
[31m+   const url = new URL([39m
[31m+     `${protocol}//${window.location.host}/api/crm-viewer/stream`[39m
[31m+   );[39m
[31m+   url.searchParams.set("session", session.viewerSessionId);[39m
[31m+   url.searchParams.set("token", session.viewerToken);[39m
[31m+   return url.toString();[39m
[31m+ }[39m

[31m+     useState("STARTING");[39m
