[31m+   const completionAttemptedRef = useRef(false);[39m
[31m+   const selected = useMemo([39m
[31m+     () =>[39m
[31m+       systems.data?.find(system => system.id === selectedId) ||[39m
[31m+       systems.data?.find(system => system.baseUrl) ||[39m
[31m+       null,[39m
[31m+     [selectedId, systems.data][39m
[31m+   );[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => {[39m
[31m+     if (selected && selected.id !== selectedId) setSelectedId(selected.id);[39m
[31m+   }, [selected, selectedId]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const onboarding = organisation.data?.settings?.onboarding;[39m
[31m+   const onboardingComplete = Boolean([39m
[31m+     onboarding &&[39m
[31m+       typeof onboarding === "object" &&[39m
[31m+       !Array.isArray(onboarding) &&[39m
[31m+       (onboarding as { complete?: unknown }).complete === true[39m
[31m+   );[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => {[39m
[31m+     setCommissioningReady(false);[39m
[31m+     setBrowserAuthenticationState("STARTING");[39m
[31m+     completionAttemptedRef.current = false;[39m
[31m+     if (!selected || !canManage || onboardingComplete) return;[39m
[31m+     let cancelled = false;[39m
[31m+     const check = async () => {[39m
[31m+       const response = await fetch([39m
[31m+         `/api/connected-system-admin/${selected.id}/commissioning`,[39m
[31m+         { credentials: "include" }[39m
[31m+       );[39m
[31m+       if (!response.ok || cancelled) return;[39m
[31m+       const body = (await response.json().catch(() => ({}))) as {[39m
[31m+         job?: { state?: string; status?: string } | null;[39m
[31m+       };[39m
[31m+       setCommissioningReady([39m
[31m+         body.job?.state === "READY" && body.job?.status === "ready"[39m
[31m+       );[39m
[31m+     };[39m
[31m+     void check();[39m
[31m+     const timer = window.setInterval(() => void check(), 3_000);[39m
[31m+     return () => {[39m
[31m+       cancelled = true;[39m
[31m+       window.clearInterval(timer);[39m
[31m+     };[39m
[31m+   }, [canManage, onboardingComplete, selected]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => {[39m
[31m+     if ([39m
[31m+       !canManage ||[39m
[31m+       onboardingComplete ||[39m
[31m+       completionAttemptedRef.current ||[39m
[31m+       browserAuthenticationState !== "AUTHENTICATED" ||[39m
[31m+       !commissioningReady ||[39m
[31m+       companySetup.data?.profile?.discoveryStatus !== "confirmed"[39m
[31m+     )[39m
[31m+       return;[39m
[31m+     completionAttemptedRef.current = true;[39m
[31m+     void completeOnboarding[39m
[31m+       .mutateAsync({ step: 4, complete: true })[39m
[31m+       .then(async () => {[39m
[31m+         await utils.organisation.current.invalidate();[39m
[31m+         toast.success("Setup complete. Your Assistant is ready.");[39m
[31m+         navigate("/assistant");[39m
[31m+       })[39m
[31m+       .catch(error => {[39m
[31m+         completionAttemptedRef.current = false;[39m
[31m+         toast.error([39m
[31m+           friendlyError([39m
[31m+             error,[39m
[31m+             "CRM commissioning is not fully proven yet, or completion could not be saved. Finish the required CRM operations and try again."[39m
[31m+           )[39m
[31m+         );[39m
[31m+       });[39m
[31m+   }, [[39m
[31m+     browserAuthenticationState,[39m
[31m+     canManage,[39m
[31m+     companySetup.data?.profile?.discoveryStatus,[39m
[31m+     completeOnboarding,[39m
[31m+     onboardingComplete,[39m
[31m+     navigate,[39m
[31m+     commissioningReady,[39m
[31m+     utils.organisation.current,[39m
[31m+   ]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   return ([39m
[31m+     <DashboardLayout>[39m
[31m+       <div[39m
[31m+         data-crm-workspace-root[39m
[31m+         className="h-[calc(100vh-66px)] min-h-0 overflow-hidden bg-[#EDF2F7]"[39m
[31m+       >[39m
[31m+         <style>{`[39m
[31m+           main:has(> [data-crm-workspace-root]) {[39m
[31m+             height: calc(100vh - 66px) !important;[39m
[31m+             min-height: 0 !important;[39m
[31m+             overflow: hidden !important;[39m
[31m+             padding: 0 !important;[39m
[31m+           }[39m
[31m+           main:has(> [data-crm-workspace-root]) > div:not([data-crm-workspace-root]) {[39m
[31m+             display: none !important;[39m
[31m+           }[39m
[31m+         `}</style>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+         {selected ? ([39m
[31m+           <LiveWorkspace[39m
[31m+             key={selected.id}[39m
[31m+             connectedSystemId={selected.id}[39m
[31m+             crmName={selected.displayName}[39m
[31m+             systems={systems.data ?? []}[39m
[31m+             onChoose={id => navigate(`/crm/${id}`)}[39m
[31m+             onToday={() => navigate("/today")}[39m
[31m+             onAuthenticationState={setBrowserAuthenticationState}[39m
[31m+           />[39m
[31m+         ) : ([39m
[31m+           <NoBrowserCrm onConnections={() => navigate("/connections")} />[39m
[31m+         )}[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+     </DashboardLayout>[39m
[31m+   );[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ function LiveWorkspace({[39m
[31m+   connectedSystemId,[39m
[31m+   crmName,[39m
[31m+   systems,[39m
[31m+   onChoose,[39m
[31m+   onToday,[39m
[31m+   onAuthenticationState,[39m
[31m+ }: {[39m
[31m+   connectedSystemId: number;[39m
[31m+   crmName: string;[39m
[31m+   systems: Array<{ id: number; displayName: string; baseUrl?: string | null }>;[39m
[31m+   onChoose: (id: number) => void;[39m
[31m+   onToday: () => void;[39m
[31m+   onAuthenticationState: (state: string) => void;[39m
[31m+ }) {[39m
[31m+   const open = trpc.crmViewer.open.useMutation();[39m
[31m+   const acquireAi = trpc.crmViewer.acquireAssistantControl.useMutation();[39m
[31m+   const releaseAi = trpc.crmViewer.releaseAssistantControl.useMutation();[39m
[31m+   const askAssistant = trpc.crmViewer.askAssistant.useMutation();[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const [session, setSession] = useState<ViewerReady | null>(null);[39m
[31m+   const [control, setControl] = useState("IDLE");[39m
[31m+   const [assistantOpen, setAssistantOpen] = useState(false);[39m
[31m+   const [image, setImage] = useState("");[39m
[31m+   const [frameMetadata, setFrameMetadata] = useState<FrameMetadata | null>([39m
[31m+     null[39m
[31m+   );[39m
[31m+   const [status, setStatus] = useState("Opening your CRM…");[39m
[31m+   const [currentUrl, setCurrentUrl] = useState("");[39m
[31m+   const [authenticationState, setAuthenticationState] = useState("STARTING");[39m
[31m+   const [assistantPrompt, setAssistantPrompt] = useState("");[39m
[31m+   const [assistantResult, setAssistantResult] = useState<string | null>(null);[39m
[31m+   const [assistantWorkflowRunId, setAssistantWorkflowRunId] = useState<number | null>(null);[39m
[31m+   const [activity, setActivity] = useState<string[]>([[39m
[31m+     "Secure CRM workspace opened",[39m
[31m+   ]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const viewerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);[39m
[31m+   const socketRef = useRef<WebSocket | null>(null);[39m
[31m+   const controlRef = useRef(control);[39m
[31m+   const pendingInputsRef = useRef<BrowserInputEvent[]>([]);[39m
[31m+   const pendingNavigationRef = useRef<BrowserNavigationAction | null>(null);[39m
[31m+   const humanControlRequestedRef = useRef(false);[39m
[31m+   const pendingAiControlRef = useRef(false);[39m
[31m+   const lastPointerMoveAtRef = useRef(0);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => {[39m
[31m+     controlRef.current = control;[39m
[31m+   }, [control]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const send = (payload: unknown) => {[39m
[31m+     if (socketRef.current?.readyState !== WebSocket.OPEN) return false;[39m
[31m+     socketRef.current.send(JSON.stringify(payload));[39m
[31m+     return true;[39m
[31m+   };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const openViewer = async (forceReconnect = false) => {[39m
[31m+     try {[39m
[31m+       socketRef.current?.close(1000, "Replacing CRM viewer session");[39m
[31m+       socketRef.current = null;[39m
[31m+       pendingInputsRef.current = [];[39m
[31m+       pendingNavigationRef.current = null;[39m
[31m+       humanControlRequestedRef.current = false;[39m
[31m+       pendingAiControlRef.current = false;[39m
[31m+       setSession(null);[39m
[31m+       setImage("");[39m
[31m+       setFrameMetadata(null);[39m
[31m+       setStatus("Opening your CRM…");[39m
[31m+       const next = await open.mutateAsync({[39m
[31m+         connectedSystemId,[39m
[31m+         forceReconnect,[39m
[31m+       });[39m
[31m+       setSession(next);[39m
[31m+       setControl(next.control);[39m
[31m+       controlRef.current = next.control;[39m
[31m+       setCurrentUrl(next.url);[39m
[31m+     } catch (error) {[39m
[31m+       setStatus(friendlyError(error, "The CRM workspace could not be opened."));[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+   };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => {[39m
[31m+     void openViewer();[39m
[31m+     return () => socketRef.current?.close();[39m
[31m+     // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps[39m
[31m+   }, [connectedSystemId]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const requestHumanControl = () => {[39m
[31m+     if (controlRef.current === "AGENT_CONTROL") return;[39m
[31m+     if (humanControlRequestedRef.current) return;[39m
[31m+     humanControlRequestedRef.current = true;[39m
[31m+     if (!send({ type: "acquireHumanControl" }))[39m
[31m+       humanControlRequestedRef.current = false;[39m
[31m+   };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const queueOrSendInput = (event: BrowserInputEvent) => {[39m
[31m+     if (controlRef.current === "AGENT_CONTROL") {[39m
[31m+       if (!(event.kind === "mouse" && event.type === "mouseMoved"))[39m
[31m+         toast.info([39m
[31m+           "AmarktAI is working in the CRM. Take control to work manually."[39m
[31m+         );[39m
[31m+       return;[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+     if (controlRef.current === "HUMAN_CONTROL") {[39m
[31m+       send({ type: "input", event });[39m
[31m+       return;[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     if (event.kind === "mouse" && event.type === "mouseMoved") {[39m
[31m+       pendingInputsRef.current = pendingInputsRef.current.filter([39m
[31m+         pending => !(pending.kind === "mouse" && pending.type === "mouseMoved")[39m
[31m+       );[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+     pendingInputsRef.current.push(event);[39m
[31m+     if (pendingInputsRef.current.length > 12)[39m
[31m+       pendingInputsRef.current.splice(0, pendingInputsRef.current.length - 12);[39m
[31m+     requestHumanControl();[39m
[31m+   };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const flushPendingInput = () => {[39m
[31m+     const queued = pendingInputsRef.current.splice(0);[39m
[31m+     for (const event of queued) send({ type: "input", event });[39m
[31m+   };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const flushPendingNavigation = () => {[39m
[31m+     const action = pendingNavigationRef.current;[39m
[31m+     pendingNavigationRef.current = null;[39m
[31m+     if (action) send({ type: "navigation", action });[39m
[31m+   };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const actuallyRequestAiControl = async () => {[39m
[31m+     if (!session) return;[39m
[31m+     pendingAiControlRef.current = false;[39m
[31m+     try {[39m
[31m+       await acquireAi.mutateAsync({ viewerSessionId: session.viewerSessionId });[39m
[31m+     } catch (error) {[39m
[31m+       toast.error([39m
[31m+         friendlyError(error, "AmarktAI could not take CRM control right now.")[39m
[31m+       );[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+   };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => {[39m
[31m+     if (!session) return;[39m
[31m+     const socket = new WebSocket(streamUrl(session));[39m
[31m+     socketRef.current = socket;[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     const sendSize = () => {[39m
[31m+       const rect = viewerRef.current?.getBoundingClientRect();[39m
[31m+       if (!rect || socket.readyState !== WebSocket.OPEN) return;[39m
[31m+       const viewport = crmDesktopViewport({[39m
[31m+         width: rect.width,[39m
[31m+         height: rect.height,[39m
[31m+       });[39m
[31m+       socket.send([39m
[31m+         JSON.stringify({[39m
[31m+           type: "resize",[39m
[31m+           width: viewport.width,[39m
[31m+           height: viewport.height,[39m
[31m+           deviceScaleFactor: 1,[39m
[31m+         })[39m
[31m+       );[39m
[31m+     };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     socket.onopen = () => {[39m
[31m+       setStatus("CRM ready for sign-in");[39m
[31m+       sendSize();[39m
[31m+     };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     socket.onmessage = event => {[39m
[31m+       const message = JSON.parse(String(event.data)) as StreamMessage;[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       if (message.type === "frame") {[39m
[31m+         setImage(`data:image/jpeg;base64,${message.data}`);[39m
[31m+         setFrameMetadata(message.metadata ?? null);[39m
[31m+       } else if (message.type === "control") {[39m
[31m+         setControl(message.control);[39m
[31m+         controlRef.current = message.control;[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+         if (message.control === "HUMAN_CONTROL") {[39m
[31m+           humanControlRequestedRef.current = false;[39m
[31m+           flushPendingInput();[39m
[31m+           flushPendingNavigation();[39m
[31m+         } else if (message.control === "IDLE") {[39m
[31m+           humanControlRequestedRef.current = false;[39m
[31m+           if (pendingAiControlRef.current) void actuallyRequestAiControl();[39m
[31m+           else if ([39m
[31m+             pendingInputsRef.current.length ||[39m
[31m+             pendingNavigationRef.current[39m
[31m+           )[39m
[31m+             requestHumanControl();[39m
[31m+         } else if (message.control === "AGENT_CONTROL") {[39m
[31m+           humanControlRequestedRef.current = false;[39m
[31m+           pendingInputsRef.current = [];[39m
[31m+           pendingNavigationRef.current = null;[39m
[31m+         }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+         setActivity(current =>[39m
[31m+           [[39m
[31m+             `${[39m
[31m+               message.control === "HUMAN_CONTROL"[39m
[31m+                 ? "You took CRM control"[39m
[31m+                 : message.control === "AGENT_CONTROL"[39m
[31m+                   ? "AmarktAI took CRM control"[39m
[31m+                   : "CRM control is idle"[39m
[31m+             } · ${new Date().toLocaleTimeString()}`,[39m
[31m+             ...current,[39m
[31m+           ].slice(0, 8)[39m
[31m+         );[39m
[31m+       } else if (message.type === "ready") {[39m
[31m+         setControl(message.control);[39m
[31m+         controlRef.current = message.control;[39m
[31m+         setCurrentUrl(message.url);[39m
[31m+       } else if (message.type === "navigation") {[39m
[31m+         setCurrentUrl(message.url);[39m
[31m+       } else if (message.type === "session") {[39m
[31m+         setCurrentUrl(message.currentUrl);[39m
[31m+         setAuthenticationState(message.authenticationState);[39m
[31m+         onAuthenticationState(message.authenticationState);[39m
[31m+         setStatus([39m
[31m+           message.errorMessage ||[39m
[31m+             (message.authenticationState === "AUTHENTICATED"[39m
[31m+               ? "Connected"[39m
[31m+               : message.authenticationState === "CHECKING"[39m
[31m+                 ? "Checking your sign-in…"[39m
[31m+                 : message.authenticationState === "MFA_OR_SSO"[39m
[31m+                   ? "Complete verification in the CRM"[39m
[31m+                   : message.authenticationState === "REAUTHENTICATION_REQUIRED"[39m
[31m+                     ? "Sign in again"[39m
[31m+                     : "Sign in directly to your CRM")[39m
[31m+         );[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+         if (message.authenticationState === "AUTHENTICATED")[39m
[31m+           setActivity(current =>[39m
[31m+             current[0]?.startsWith("CRM authenticated")[39m
[31m+               ? current[39m
[31m+               : [[39m
[31m+                   `CRM authenticated · ${new Date().toLocaleTimeString()}`,[39m
[31m+                   ...current,[39m
[31m+                 ].slice(0, 8)[39m
[31m+           );[39m
[31m+       } else if (message.type === "disconnected") {[39m
[31m+         setStatus("The CRM browser connection paused. Reopen it to continue.");[39m
[31m+       } else if (message.type === "error") {[39m
[31m+         const friendly = friendlyError([39m
[31m+           message.message || message.code,[39m
[31m+           "That CRM action could not be completed."[39m
[31m+         );[39m
[31m+         setStatus(friendly);[39m
[31m+         toast.error(friendly);[39m
[31m+       }[39m
[31m+     };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     socket.onclose = event => {[39m
[31m+       setStatus([39m
[31m+         event.code === 4002[39m
[31m+           ? "This CRM was disconnected. Reconnect it from Connections to continue."[39m
[31m+           : "Connection paused. Reconnect to continue."[39m
[31m+       );[39m
[31m+       pendingInputsRef.current = [];[39m
[31m+       pendingNavigationRef.current = null;[39m
[31m+       humanControlRequestedRef.current = false;[39m
[31m+     };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     const observer = new ResizeObserver(sendSize);[39m
[31m+     if (viewerRef.current) observer.observe(viewerRef.current);[39m
[31m+     const heartbeat = window.setInterval(() => {[39m
[31m+       if (socket.readyState === WebSocket.OPEN && !document.hidden)[39m
[31m+         socket.send(JSON.stringify({ type: "ping" }));[39m
[31m+     }, 25_000);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     return () => {[39m
[31m+       window.clearInterval(heartbeat);[39m
[31m+       observer.disconnect();[39m
[31m+       socket.close();[39m
[31m+     };[39m
[31m+     // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps[39m
[31m+   }, [session]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => {[39m
[31m+     const onVisibility = () =>[39m
[31m+       send({ type: "visibility", visible: !document.hidden });[39m
[31m+     document.addEventListener("visibilitychange", onVisibility);[39m
[31m+     onVisibility();[39m
[31m+     return () => document.removeEventListener("visibilitychange", onVisibility);[39m
[31m+     // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps[39m
[31m+   }, [session]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const mapPointer = (clientX: number, clientY: number) => {[39m
[31m+     const rect = viewerRef.current?.getBoundingClientRect();[39m
[31m+     if (!rect) return null;[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     const sourceWidth =[39m
[31m+       frameMetadata?.deviceWidth && frameMetadata.deviceWidth > 0[39m
[31m+         ? frameMetadata.deviceWidth[39m
[31m+         : rect.width;[39m
[31m+     const sourceHeight =[39m
[31m+       frameMetadata?.deviceHeight && frameMetadata.deviceHeight > 0[39m
[31m+         ? frameMetadata.deviceHeight[39m
[31m+         : rect.height;[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     const scale = Math.min([39m
[31m+       rect.width / sourceWidth,[39m
[31m+       rect.height / sourceHeight[39m
[31m+     );[39m
[31m+     const renderedWidth = sourceWidth * scale;[39m
[31m+     const renderedHeight = sourceHeight * scale;[39m
[31m+     const offsetX = (rect.width - renderedWidth) / 2;[39m
[31m+     const offsetY = (rect.height - renderedHeight) / 2;[39m
[31m+     const localX = clientX - rect.left - offsetX;[39m
[31m+     const localY = clientY - rect.top - offsetY;[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     if ([39m
[31m+       localX < 0 ||[39m
[31m+       localY < 0 ||[39m
[31m+       localX > renderedWidth ||[39m
[31m+       localY > renderedHeight[39m
[31m+     )[39m
[31m+       return null;[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     return {[39m
[31m+       x: localX / scale,[39m
[31m+       y: localY / scale,[39m
[31m+     };[39m
[31m+   };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const forwardPointer = ([39m
[31m+     event: PointerEvent<HTMLDivElement>,[39m
[31m+     type: "mousePressed" | "mouseReleased" | "mouseMoved"[39m
[31m+   ) => {[39m
[31m+     if (type === "mouseMoved") {[39m
[31m+       if (controlRef.current === "AGENT_CONTROL") return;[39m
[31m+       const now = performance.now();[39m
[31m+       if (now - lastPointerMoveAtRef.current < 30) return;[39m
[31m+       lastPointerMoveAtRef.current = now;[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+     const point = mapPointer(event.clientX, event.clientY);[39m
[31m+     if (!point) return;[39m
[31m+     if (type === "mousePressed")[39m
[31m+       viewerRef.current?.focus({ preventScroll: true });[39m
[31m+     queueOrSendInput({[39m
[31m+       kind: "mouse",[39m
[31m+       type,[39m
[31m+       ...point,[39m
[31m+       button:[39m
[31m+         event.button === 2[39m
[31m+           ? "right"[39m
[31m+           : event.button === 1[39m
[31m+             ? "middle"[39m
[31m+             : event.button === 0[39m
[31m+               ? "left"[39m
[31m+               : "none",[39m
[31m+       clickCount: Math.max(1, event.detail || 1),[39m
[31m+     });[39m
[31m+   };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const forwardWheel = (event: WheelEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {[39m
[31m+     event.preventDefault();[39m
[31m+     const point = mapPointer(event.clientX, event.clientY);[39m
[31m+     const rect = viewerRef.current?.getBoundingClientRect();[39m
[31m+     if (!point || !rect) return;[39m
[31m+     queueOrSendInput({[39m
[31m+       kind: "mouse",[39m
[31m+       type: "mouseWheel",[39m
[31m+       ...point,[39m
[31m+       deltaX: normalizeCrmWheelDelta(event.deltaX, event.deltaMode, rect.width),[39m
[31m+       deltaY: normalizeCrmWheelDelta([39m
[31m+         event.deltaY,[39m
[31m+         event.deltaMode,[39m
[31m+         rect.height[39m
[31m+       ),[39m
[31m+     });[39m
[31m+   };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const forwardKey = ([39m
[31m+     event: KeyboardEvent<HTMLDivElement>,[39m
[31m+     type: "keyDown" | "keyUp"[39m
[31m+   ) => {[39m
[31m+     const commandKey = event.metaKey || event.ctrlKey;[39m
[31m+     const key = event.key.toLowerCase();[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     // Keep browser/app-level shortcuts outside the remote page. Paste is the[39m
[31m+     // exception: allow the browser to emit the ClipboardEvent, which is then[39m
[31m+     // forwarded through the bounded Input.insertText path below.[39m
[31m+     if (commandKey && ["l", "r", "t", "n", "q", "w"].includes(key)) return;[39m
[31m+     if (commandKey && key === "v") return;[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     event.preventDefault();[39m
[31m+     const modifiers =[39m
[31m+       (event.altKey ? 1 : 0) |[39m
[31m+       (event.ctrlKey ? 2 : 0) |[39m
[31m+       (event.metaKey ? 4 : 0) |[39m
[31m+       (event.shiftKey ? 8 : 0);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     queueOrSendInput({[39m
[31m+       kind: "key",[39m
[31m+       type,[39m
[31m+       key: event.key,[39m
[31m+       code: event.code,[39m
[31m+       text:[39m
[31m+         type === "keyDown" &&[39m
[31m+         event.key.length === 1 &&[39m
[31m+         !event.ctrlKey &&[39m
[31m+         !event.metaKey[39m
[31m+           ? event.key[39m
[31m+           : "",[39m
[31m+       modifiers,[39m
[31m+     });[39m
[31m+   };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const forwardPaste = (event: ClipboardEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {[39m
[31m+     event.preventDefault();[39m
[31m+     const text = event.clipboardData.getData("text").slice(0, 4_000);[39m
[31m+     if (!text) return;[39m
[31m+     queueOrSendInput({[39m
[31m+       kind: "key",[39m
[31m+       type: "keyDown",[39m
[31m+       key: "",[39m
[31m+       code: "",[39m
[31m+       text,[39m
[31m+       modifiers: 0,[39m
[31m+     });[39m
[31m+   };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const takeControl = async () => {[39m
[31m+     try {[39m
[31m+       if (controlRef.current === "AGENT_CONTROL" && session) {[39m
[31m+         await releaseAi.mutateAsync({[39m
[31m+           viewerSessionId: session.viewerSessionId,[39m
[31m+         });[39m
[31m+         // The mutation has synchronously released the server arbitration lock.[39m
[31m+         // Do not wait on a websocket IDLE frame before asking for human control.[39m
[31m+         controlRef.current = "IDLE";[39m
[31m+         setControl("IDLE");[39m
[31m+         humanControlRequestedRef.current = false;[39m
[31m+       }[39m
[31m+       requestHumanControl();[39m
[31m+       viewerRef.current?.focus({ preventScroll: true });[39m
[31m+     } catch (error) {[39m
[31m+       toast.error([39m
[31m+         friendlyError(error, "CRM control could not be returned to you.")[39m
[31m+       );[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+   };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const requestAi = () => {[39m
[31m+     if (!session || controlRef.current === "AGENT_CONTROL") return;[39m
[31m+     pendingAiControlRef.current = true;[39m
[31m+     pendingNavigationRef.current = null;[39m
[31m+     if (controlRef.current === "HUMAN_CONTROL")[39m
[31m+       send({ type: "releaseHumanControl" });[39m
[31m+     else void actuallyRequestAiControl();[39m
[31m+   };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const navigateBrowser = (action: BrowserNavigationAction) => {[39m
[31m+     if (controlRef.current === "AGENT_CONTROL") {[39m
[31m+       toast.info([39m
[31m+         "AmarktAI is working in the CRM. Take control before navigating."[39m
[31m+       );[39m
[31m+       return;[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+     if (controlRef.current === "HUMAN_CONTROL") {[39m
[31m+       send({ type: "navigation", action });[39m
[31m+       return;[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+     pendingNavigationRef.current = action;[39m
[31m+     requestHumanControl();[39m
[31m+   };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const confirmSignedIn = () => send({ type: "customerFinishedSigningIn" });[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const submitAssistant = async () => {[39m
[31m+     if (!session || !assistantPrompt.trim()) return;[39m
[31m+     try {[39m
[31m+       setAssistantWorkflowRunId(null);[39m
[31m+       const result = await askAssistant.mutateAsync({[39m
[31m+         viewerSessionId: session.viewerSessionId,[39m
[31m+         command: assistantPrompt.trim(),[39m
[31m+       });[39m
[31m+       setAssistantResult(result.summary);[39m
[31m+       setAssistantWorkflowRunId([39m
[31m+         typeof result.workflowRunId === "number" ? result.workflowRunId : null[39m
[31m+       );[39m
[31m+       setAssistantPrompt("");[39m
[31m+     } catch (error) {[39m
[31m+       setAssistantWorkflowRunId(null);[39m
[31m+       setAssistantResult([39m
[31m+         friendlyError(error, "AmarktAI could not complete that request.")[39m
[31m+       );[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+   };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const domain = (() => {[39m
[31m+     try {[39m
[31m+       return new URL(currentUrl).hostname;[39m
[31m+     } catch {[39m
[31m+       return crmName;[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+   })();[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const authReady = authenticationState === "AUTHENTICATED";[39m
[31m+   const availableSystems = systems.filter(system => system.baseUrl);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   return ([39m
[31m+     <div className="flex h-full min-h-0 flex-col bg-white">[39m
[31m+       <header className="flex h-12 shrink-0 items-center gap-2 border-b border-[#D7E0EA] bg-[#F7F9FC] px-2.5">[39m
[31m+         <Button[39m
[31m+           variant="ghost"[39m
[31m+           size="sm"[39m
[31m+           className="h-8 px-2 text-[#526277]"[39m
[31m+           onClick={onToday}[39m
[31m+         >[39m
[31m+           <ChevronLeft className="mr-1 h-4 w-4" />[39m
[31m+           <span className="hidden xl:inline">Today</span>[39m
[31m+         </Button>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+         <div className="h-6 w-px bg-[#D7E0EA]" />[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+         <div className="flex items-center gap-0.5">[39m
[31m+           <Button[39m
[31m+             aria-label="Back"[39m
[31m+             variant="ghost"[39m
[31m+             size="sm"[39m
[31m+             className="h-8 w-8 p-0"[39m
[31m+             onClick={() => navigateBrowser("back")}[39m
[31m+           >[39m
[31m+             <ArrowLeft className="h-4 w-4" />[39m
[31m+           </Button>[39m
[31m+           <Button[39m
[31m+             aria-label="Forward"[39m
[31m+             variant="ghost"[39m
[31m+             size="sm"[39m
[31m+             className="h-8 w-8 p-0"[39m
[31m+             onClick={() => navigateBrowser("forward")}[39m
[31m+           >[39m
[31m+             <ArrowRight className="h-4 w-4" />[39m
[31m+           </Button>[39m
[31m+           <Button[39m
[31m+             aria-label="Refresh CRM"[39m
[31m+             variant="ghost"[39m
[31m+             size="sm"[39m
[31m+             className="h-8 w-8 p-0"[39m
[31m+             onClick={() => navigateBrowser("refresh")}[39m
[31m+           >[39m
[31m+             <RefreshCw className="h-4 w-4" />[39m
[31m+           </Button>[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+         <div className="flex min-w-0 flex-1 items-center gap-2 rounded-lg border border-[#D7E0EA] bg-white px-3 py-1.5 text-xs shadow-sm">[39m
[31m+           <Globe2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0 text-[#3F70D8]" />[39m
[31m+           {availableSystems.length > 1 ? ([39m
[31m+             <select[39m
[31m+               aria-label="Choose CRM"[39m
[31m+               value={connectedSystemId}[39m
[31m+               onChange={event => onChoose(Number(event.target.value))}[39m
[31m+               className="max-w-40 bg-transparent font-bold text-[#26354A] outline-none"[39m
[31m+             >[39m
[31m+               {availableSystems.map(system => ([39m
[31m+                 <option key={system.id} value={system.id}>[39m
[31m+                   {system.displayName}[39m
[31m+                 </option>[39m
[31m+               ))}[39m
[31m+             </select>[39m
[31m+           ) : ([39m
[31m+             <span className="shrink-0 font-bold text-[#26354A]">{crmName}</span>[39m
[31m+           )}[39m
[31m+           <span className="truncate text-[#77859A]">{domain}</span>[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+         <div className="hidden min-w-0 items-center gap-2 lg:flex">[39m
[31m+           <span[39m
[31m+             className={`h-2 w-2 shrink-0 rounded-full ${[39m
[31m+               authReady ? "bg-emerald-500" : "bg-amber-400"[39m
[31m+             }`}[39m
[31m+           />[39m
[31m+           <span className="max-w-52 truncate text-xs font-semibold text-[#526277]">[39m
[31m+             {status}[39m
[31m+           </span>[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+         <Button[39m
[31m+           variant={assistantOpen ? "default" : "outline"}[39m
[31m+           size="sm"[39m
[31m+           className="h-8"[39m
[31m+           onClick={() => setAssistantOpen(open => !open)}[39m
[31m+         >[39m
[31m+           <Sparkles className="mr-1.5 h-4 w-4" />[39m
[31m+           Assistant[39m
[31m+         </Button>[39m
[31m+       </header>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       <div className="relative flex min-h-0 flex-1">[39m
[31m+         <section className="relative min-w-0 flex-1 bg-[#E9EEF5]">[39m
[31m+           <div[39m
[31m+             ref={viewerRef}[39m
[31m+             role="application"[39m
[31m+             aria-label={`${crmName} live CRM`}[39m
[31m+             tabIndex={0}[39m
[31m+             onPointerEnter={() => requestHumanControl()}[39m
[31m+             onPointerDown={event => forwardPointer(event, "mousePressed")}[39m
[31m+             onPointerUp={event => forwardPointer(event, "mouseReleased")}[39m
[31m+             onPointerMove={event => forwardPointer(event, "mouseMoved")}[39m
[31m+             onWheel={forwardWheel}[39m
[31m+             onKeyDown={event => forwardKey(event, "keyDown")}[39m
[31m+             onKeyUp={event => forwardKey(event, "keyUp")}[39m
[31m+             onPaste={forwardPaste}[39m
[31m+             onContextMenu={event => event.preventDefault()}[39m
[31m+             className="relative h-full w-full cursor-default overflow-hidden bg-[#E9EEF5] outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-inset focus-visible:ring-[#3F70D8]"[39m
[31m+           >[39m
[31m+             {image ? ([39m
[31m+               <img[39m
[31m+                 src={image}[39m
[31m+                 alt=""[39m
[31m+                 aria-hidden="true"[39m
[31m+                 draggable={false}[39m
[31m+                 className="pointer-events-none h-full w-full select-none object-contain"[39m
[31m+               />[39m
[31m+             ) : ([39m
[31m+               <div className="flex h-full flex-col items-center justify-center gap-3 text-[#6C798B]">[39m
[31m+                 <Loader2 className="h-7 w-7 animate-spin text-[#3F70D8]" />[39m
[31m+                 <p className="text-sm font-semibold">{status}</p>[39m
[31m+               </div>[39m
[31m+             )}[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+             <div className="pointer-events-none absolute bottom-3 left-3 flex items-center gap-2 rounded-full border border-white/60 bg-[#26354A]/90 px-3 py-1.5 text-[11px] font-bold text-white shadow-md backdrop-blur">[39m
[31m+               <MousePointer2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />[39m
[31m+               {control === "HUMAN_CONTROL"[39m
[31m+                 ? "You control the CRM"[39m
[31m+                 : control === "AGENT_CONTROL"[39m
[31m+                   ? "AmarktAI is working"[39m
[31m+                   : "Move here to take control"}[39m
[31m+             </div>[39m
[31m+           </div>[39m
[31m+         </section>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+         {assistantOpen ? ([39m
[31m+           <aside className="absolute inset-y-0 right-0 z-30 flex w-[min(390px,94vw)] flex-col border-l border-[#D7E0EA] bg-white shadow-[-18px_0_45px_rgba(38,53,74,.12)] lg:relative lg:z-auto lg:w-[370px] lg:shadow-none">[39m
[31m+             <div className="flex h-12 shrink-0 items-center justify-between border-b border-[#D7E0EA] px-4">[39m
[31m+               <div className="flex items-center gap-2">[39m
[31m+                 <Sparkles className="h-4 w-4 text-[#3F70D8]" />[39m
[31m+                 <div>[39m
[31m+                   <p className="text-sm font-bold text-[#26354A]">[39m
[31m+                     AmarktAI Assistant[39m
[31m+                   </p>[39m
[31m+                   <p className="text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[.12em] text-emerald-600">[39m
[31m+                     Ready[39m
[31m+                   </p>[39m
[31m+                 </div>[39m
[31m+               </div>[39m
[31m+               <Button[39m
[31m+                 variant="ghost"[39m
[31m+                 size="sm"[39m
[31m+                 className="h-8 w-8 p-0"[39m
[31m+                 aria-label="Close assistant"[39m
[31m+                 onClick={() => setAssistantOpen(false)}[39m
[31m+               >[39m
[31m+                 <X className="h-4 w-4" />[39m
[31m+               </Button>[39m
[31m+             </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+             <div className="min-h-0 flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-4">[39m
[31m+               <div className="rounded-xl border border-[#DCE7F6] bg-[#F4F8FF] p-3">[39m
[31m+                 <p className="text-xs font-bold text-[#26354A]">{status}</p>[39m
[31m+                 <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-5 text-[#6C798B]">[39m
[31m+                   {authReady[39m
[31m+                     ? "Your private CRM session is connected. Ask me to work with the customer or use the CRM yourself."[39m
[31m+                     : "Finish signing in inside the CRM. Your password and verification code stay between you and the CRM."}[39m
[31m+                 </p>[39m
[31m+               </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+               {assistantResult ? ([39m
[31m+                 <div className="mt-3 rounded-xl border border-[#D7E0EA] bg-white p-3 text-sm leading-6 text-[#33445B]">[39m
[31m+                   {assistantResult}[39m
[31m+                 </div>[39m
[31m+               ) : null}[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+               <InlineCrmReview workflowRunId={assistantWorkflowRunId} />[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+               <details className="mt-4 rounded-xl border border-[#D7E0EA] bg-white">[39m
[31m+                 <summary className="cursor-pointer list-none px-3 py-2.5 text-xs font-bold text-[#33445B]">[39m
[31m+                   CRM controls[39m
[31m+                 </summary>[39m
[31m+                 <div className="space-y-2 border-t border-[#E5EAF0] p-3">[39m
[31m+                   <Button[39m
[31m+                     variant="outline"[39m
[31m+                     size="sm"[39m
[31m+                     className="w-full justify-start"[39m
[31m+                     onClick={() => void takeControl()}[39m
[31m+                   >[39m
[31m+                     <MousePointer2 className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" />[39m
[31m+                     {control === "HUMAN_CONTROL"[39m
[31m+                       ? "You have control"[39m
[31m+                       : "Take control"}[39m
[31m+                   </Button>[39m
[31m+                   <Button[39m
[31m+                     variant="outline"[39m
[31m+                     size="sm"[39m
[31m+                     className="w-full justify-start"[39m
[31m+                     onClick={requestAi}[39m
[31m+                     disabled={[39m
[31m+                       control === "AGENT_CONTROL" || acquireAi.isPending[39m
[31m+                     }[39m
[31m+                   >[39m
[31m+                     <ShieldCheck className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" />[39m
[31m+                     {control === "AGENT_CONTROL"[39m
[31m+                       ? "AmarktAI has control"[39m
[31m+                       : "Give control to AmarktAI"}[39m
[31m+                   </Button>[39m
[31m+                   {!authReady ? ([39m
[31m+                     <Button[39m
[31m+                       size="sm"[39m
[31m+                       className="w-full justify-start"[39m
[31m+                       onClick={confirmSignedIn}[39m
[31m+                     >[39m
[31m+                       Check my sign-in[39m
[31m+                     </Button>[39m
[31m+                   ) : null}[39m
[31m+                   <Button[39m
[31m+                     variant="ghost"[39m
[31m+                     size="sm"[39m
[31m+                     className="w-full justify-start"[39m
[31m+                     onClick={() => void openViewer(true)}[39m
[31m+                     disabled={open.isPending}[39m
[31m+                   >[39m
[31m+                     <RefreshCw[39m
[31m+                       className={`mr-2 h-4 w-4 ${[39m
[31m+                         open.isPending ? "animate-spin" : ""[39m
[31m+                       }`}[39m
[31m+                     />[39m
[31m+                     Reconnect browser[39m
[31m+                   </Button>[39m
[31m+                 </div>[39m
[31m+               </details>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+               <details className="mt-3 rounded-xl border border-[#D7E0EA] bg-white">[39m
[31m+                 <summary className="cursor-pointer list-none px-3 py-2.5 text-xs font-bold text-[#33445B]">[39m
[31m+                   Recent CRM activity[39m
[31m+                 </summary>[39m
[31m+                 <ul className="space-y-2 border-t border-[#E5EAF0] p-3 text-xs text-[#6C798B]">[39m
[31m+                   {activity.map((item, index) => ([39m
[31m+                     <li key={`${item}-${index}`}>{item}</li>[39m
[31m+                   ))}[39m
[31m+                 </ul>[39m
[31m+               </details>[39m
[31m+             </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+             <div className="shrink-0 border-t border-[#D7E0EA] bg-[#FAFBFD] p-3">[39m
[31m+               <textarea[39m
[31m+                 aria-label="Ask AmarktAI"[39m
[31m+                 value={assistantPrompt}[39m
[31m+                 onChange={event => setAssistantPrompt(event.target.value)}[39m
[31m+                 onKeyDown={event => {[39m
[31m+                   if (event.key === "Enter" && !event.shiftKey) {[39m
[31m+                     event.preventDefault();[39m
[31m+                     void submitAssistant();[39m
[31m+                   }[39m
[31m+                 }}[39m
[31m+                 className="min-h-20 w-full resize-none rounded-xl border border-[#CCD6E2] bg-white px-3 py-2 text-sm outline-none focus:border-[#3F70D8] focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#DCE7F6]"[39m
[31m+                 placeholder="Ask AmarktAI to find a customer, prepare a call, explain history…"[39m
[31m+               />[39m
[31m+               <div className="mt-2 flex items-center justify-between gap-2">[39m
[31m+                 <span className="text-[10px] text-[#8995A6]">[39m
[31m+                   Enter to send · Shift+Enter for a new line[39m
[31m+                 </span>[39m
[31m+                 <Button[39m
[31m+                   size="sm"[39m
[31m+                   onClick={() => void submitAssistant()}[39m
[31m+                   disabled={!assistantPrompt.trim() || askAssistant.isPending}[39m
[31m+                 >[39m
[31m+                   {askAssistant.isPending ? "Working…" : "Ask"}[39m
[31m+                 </Button>[39m
[31m+               </div>[39m
[31m+             </div>[39m
[31m+           </aside>[39m
[31m+         ) : null}[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       <div className="sr-only" aria-live="polite">[39m
[31m+         {control === "HUMAN_CONTROL"[39m
[31m+           ? "You currently control the CRM."[39m
[31m+           : status}[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+     </div>[39m
[31m+   );[39m
[31m+ }[39m

  (location === "/company-setup" && profileConfirmed));%0A+%0A+   return (%0A+     <SidebarProvider>%0A+       <Sidebar%0A+         collapsible="icon"%0A+         className="amarktai-dashboard-sidebar
